let _memoryStorage = {};
const SafeStorage = {
    getItem(k) {
        try { return localStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return _memoryStorage[k] || null; }
    },
    setItem(k, v) {
        try { localStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { _memoryStorage[k] = v; }
    },
    removeItem(k) {
        try { localStorage.removeItem(k); } catch (e) { delete _memoryStorage[k]; }
    }
};

let _memorySession = {};
const SafeSession = {
    getItem(k) {
        try { return sessionStorage.getItem(k); } catch (e) { return _memorySession[k] || null; }
    },
    setItem(k, v) {
        try { sessionStorage.setItem(k, v); } catch (e) { _memorySession[k] = v; }
    },
    removeItem(k) {
        try { sessionStorage.removeItem(k); } catch (e) { delete _memorySession[k]; }
    }
};

/**
 * STORAGE MANAGER (Local Storage Abstraction)
 * Mendukung kustomisasi sekolah oleh Super Admin, daftar guru dinamis, kata mutiara,
 * kelola prestasi global dengan foto file, dan kelola agenda kegiatan kesiswaan.
 */

const STORAGE_KEY = "kesiswaan_app_data_v2";

const StorageManager = {
    init() {
        const stored = SafeStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!stored) {
            this.saveData({
                config: DEFAULT_CONFIG,
                quotes: DEFAULT_QUOTES,
                teachers: DEFAULT_TEACHERS,
                students: INITIAL_STUDENTS,
                rules: DEFAULT_RULES,
                suratTemplate: DEFAULT_SURAT_TEMPLATE,
                violationsMaster: MASTER_PELANGGARAN,
                achievementsMaster: MASTER_PRESTASI,
                activities: DAFTAR_KEGIATAN
            });
        }
    },

    getData() {
        const data = SafeStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        if (!data) {
            this.init();
            try { return JSON.parse(SafeStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)) || {}; } catch (e) { return {}; }
        }
        try {
            return JSON.parse(data);
        } catch (e) {
            this.resetToDefault();
            try { return JSON.parse(SafeStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY)) || {}; } catch (err) { return {}; }
        }
    },

    saveData(data) {
        try {
            SafeStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
        } catch (e) {
            console.error("Gagal menyimpan ke storage:", e);
            App.showToast("Penyimpanan lokal penuh. Harap gunakan gambar berukuran lebih kecil.");
        }
    },

    // PENGATURAN SEKOLAH (SUPER ADMIN)
    getConfig() {
        const stored = this.getData().config || {};
        const config = { ...DEFAULT_CONFIG, ...stored };
        if (!config.dashboardBgUrl || config.dashboardBgUrl === "none" || config.dashboardBgUrl.trim() === "" || config.dashboardBgUrl.includes("photo-1541829070764-84a7d30dd3f3")) {
            config.dashboardBgUrl = DEFAULT_CONFIG.dashboardBgUrl;
        }
        return config;
    },

    updateConfig(newConfig) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.config = { ...data.config, ...newConfig };
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.config;
    },

    // KATA-KATA MUTIARA / MOTIVASI (SUPER ADMIN)
    getQuotes() {
        return this.getData().quotes || DEFAULT_QUOTES;
    },

    addQuote(quoteData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.quotes) data.quotes = [];
        data.quotes.unshift({
            quote: quoteData.quote,
            tokoh: quoteData.tokoh || "Pesan Kesiswaan",
            warna: quoteData.warna || "ocean",
            font: quoteData.font || "playfair",
            efek: quoteData.efek || "all",
            kategori: quoteData.kategori || "💡 Kata Mutiara & Motivasi Hari Ini",
            customColor: quoteData.customColor || ""
        });
        this.saveData(data);
    },

    deleteQuote(index) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (data.quotes && data.quotes.length > 1) {
            data.quotes.splice(index, 1);
            this.saveData(data);
        }
    },

    // KELOLA GURU / KESISWAAN (SUPER ADMIN)
    getTeachers() {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.teachers || !Array.isArray(data.teachers) || data.teachers.length === 0 || !data.teachers.some(t => t.username.toLowerCase() === "admin")) {
            data.teachers = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_TEACHERS));
            this.saveData(data);
        }
        return data.teachers;
    },

    getTeacherByUsername(username) {
        const teachers = this.getTeachers();
        return teachers.find(t => t.username.toLowerCase() === username.toLowerCase()) || null;
    },

    addTeacher(teacherData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.teachers) data.teachers = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_TEACHERS));
        const exists = data.teachers.some(t => t.username.toLowerCase() === teacherData.username.toLowerCase());
        if (exists) throw new Error("Username sudah digunakan guru lain!");

        data.teachers.push({
            username: teacherData.username.trim(),
            password: teacherData.password || "123",
            name: teacherData.name.trim(),
            role: teacherData.role || "guru",
            roleLabel: teacherData.roleLabel || "Staf Kesiswaan",
            sekbid: teacherData.sekbid || "-",
            pokja: teacherData.pokja || "-",
            icon: teacherData.icon || (teacherData.role === "superadmin" ? "👑" : "👨‍🏫")
        });
        this.saveData(data);
        return true;
    },

    updateTeacher(username, updatedData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.teachers) data.teachers = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_TEACHERS));
        const index = data.teachers.findIndex(t => t.username.toLowerCase() === username.toLowerCase());
        if (index === -1) throw new Error("Data guru tidak ditemukan!");

        const current = data.teachers[index];
        const newRole = updatedData.role !== undefined ? updatedData.role : current.role;
        const newRoleLabel = updatedData.roleLabel ? updatedData.roleLabel.trim() : current.roleLabel;

        data.teachers[index] = {
            ...current,
            name: updatedData.name ? updatedData.name.trim() : current.name,
            role: newRole,
            roleLabel: newRoleLabel,
            sekbid: updatedData.sekbid !== undefined ? updatedData.sekbid.trim() : (current.sekbid || "-"),
            pokja: updatedData.pokja !== undefined ? updatedData.pokja.trim() : (current.pokja || "-"),
            password: updatedData.password ? updatedData.password.trim() : current.password,
            icon: updatedData.icon || (newRole === "superadmin" ? "👑" : (current.icon || "👨‍🏫"))
        };
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.teachers[index];
    },

    deleteTeacher(username) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (username === "admin") throw new Error("Akun Super Admin utama tidak boleh dihapus!");
        data.teachers = (data.teachers || []).filter(t => t.username !== username);
        this.saveData(data);
        return true;
    },

    // KELOLA KEGIATAN KESISWAAN (ADMIN & SUPER ADMIN)
    getActivities() {
        return this.getData().activities || DAFTAR_KEGIATAN;
    },

    addActivity(activityData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.activities) data.activities = [];
        const newAct = {
            id: "ACT-" + Date.now(),
            nama: activityData.nama,
            tanggal: activityData.tanggal,
            kategori: activityData.kategori || "Kegiatan Sekolah",
            tempat: activityData.tempat || "Lingkungan Sekolah",
            keterangan: activityData.keterangan || "",
            foto: activityData.foto || ""
        };
        data.activities.unshift(newAct);
        this.saveData(data);
        return newAct;
    },

    deleteActivity(activityId) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.activities = (data.activities || []).filter(a => a.id !== activityId);
        this.saveData(data);
        return true;
    },

    // MASTER JENIS PRESTASI & POIN
    getAchievementsMaster() {
        return this.getData().achievementsMaster || MASTER_PRESTASI;
    },

    // MASTER JENIS PELANGGARAN & POIN (SUPER ADMIN)
    getViolationsMaster() {
        return this.getData().violationsMaster || MASTER_PELANGGARAN;
    },

    addViolationMaster(violationData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.violationsMaster) data.violationsMaster = [...MASTER_PELANGGARAN];
        const newV = {
            id: "P" + (Date.now().toString().slice(-4)),
            nama: violationData.nama,
            kategori: violationData.kategori || "Kedisiplinan",
            poin: Number(violationData.poin) || 5
        };
        data.violationsMaster.push(newV);
        this.saveData(data);
        return newV;
    },

    deleteViolationMaster(id) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.violationsMaster = (data.violationsMaster || MASTER_PELANGGARAN).filter(v => v.id !== id);
        this.saveData(data);
        return true;
    },

    // ATURAN RENTANG POIN & SANKSI OTOMATIS (SUPER ADMIN)
    getRules() {
        return this.getData().rules || DEFAULT_RULES;
    },

    updateRules(newRules) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.rules = newRules;
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.rules;
    },

    // TEMPLATE SURAT PANGGILAN ORANG TUA (SUPER ADMIN)
    getSuratTemplate() {
        const data = this.getData();
        return data.suratTemplate || DEFAULT_SURAT_TEMPLATE;
    },

    updateSuratTemplate(newTemplate) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.suratTemplate = { ...(data.suratTemplate || DEFAULT_SURAT_TEMPLATE), ...newTemplate };
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.suratTemplate;
    },

    // CEK APAKAH SISWA SUDAH MENCAPAI BATAS KADAR SP UNTUK SURAT PANGGILAN ORTU (>= 31 Poin)
    isStudentEligibleForSummons(student) {
        if (!student) return false;
        const status = this.calculateStudentStatus(student);
        // Batas Poin SP adalah >= 31 (dimulai dari Panggilan Orang Tua I / SP 1)
        return status.totalPoinPelanggaran >= 31;
    },

    getRuleForPoints(points) {
        const rules = this.getRules();
        for (const rule of rules) {
            if (points >= rule.minPoint && points <= rule.maxPoint) {
                return rule;
            }
        }
        return rules[0] || { minPoint: 0, maxPoint: 15, status: "Aman", action: "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas", badgeClass: "pill-green" };
    },

    // TEMPLATE SURAT PANGGILAN ORANG TUA (SUPER ADMIN)
    getSuratTemplate() {
        return this.getData().suratTemplate || DEFAULT_SURAT_TEMPLATE;
    },

    updateSuratTemplate(newTemplate) {
        const data = this.getData();
        data.suratTemplate = newTemplate;
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.suratTemplate;
    },

    isStudentEligibleForSummons(student) {
        if (!student) return false;
        const st = this.calculateStudentStatus(student);
        // Batas poin SP: minimal 31 Poin (Panggilan Ortu I / SP 1 / SP 2 / SP 3)
        return (st.totalPoinPelanggaran || 0) >= 31;
    },

    // DATA PRESTASI GLOBAL (UNTUK KELOLA PRESTASI DI ADMIN)
    getAllAchievements() {
        const students = this.getStudents();
        let all = [];
        students.forEach(s => {
            (s.prestasi || []).forEach(p => {
                all.push({
                    ...p,
                    siswaId: s.id,
                    siswaNama: s.nama,
                    siswaKelas: s.kelas,
                    siswaFoto: s.foto
                });
            });
        });
        return all.sort((a, b) => (b.poin || 0) - (a.poin || 0));
    },

    deleteAchievement(achievementId) {
        const data = this.getData();
        let found = false;
        (data.students || []).forEach(s => {
            if (s.prestasi) {
                const prevLen = s.prestasi.length;
                s.prestasi = s.prestasi.filter(p => p.id !== achievementId);
                if (s.prestasi.length < prevLen) found = true;
            }
        });
        if (found) this.saveData(data);
        return found;
    },

    // DATA SISWA
    getStudents() {
        return this.getData().students || [];
    },

    getStudentById(id) {
        return this.getStudents().find(s => s.id === id) || null;
    },

    getStudentByNisn(nisn) {
        if (!nisn) return null;
        const clean = nisn.trim();
        return this.getStudents().find(s => s.nisn === clean) || null;
    },

    calculateStudentStatus(student) {
        const totalPoinPelanggaran = (student.pelanggaran || []).reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr.poin) || 0), 0);
        const totalPoinPrestasi = (student.prestasi || []).reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr.poin) || 0), 0);
        
        const rules = this.getData().rules || DEFAULT_RULES;
        let ruleMatch = rules[0];

        for (const rule of rules) {
            if (totalPoinPelanggaran >= rule.minPoint && totalPoinPelanggaran <= rule.maxPoint) {
                ruleMatch = rule;
                break;
            }
        }

        return {
            totalPoinPelanggaran,
            totalPoinPrestasi,
            saldoPoinKebaikan: Math.max(0, 100 - totalPoinPelanggaran + Math.floor(totalPoinPrestasi * 0.5)),
            statusText: ruleMatch ? ruleMatch.status : "Aman",
            actionText: ruleMatch ? ruleMatch.action : "Tidak ada sanksi",
            badgeClass: ruleMatch ? ruleMatch.badgeClass : "pill-green",
            isBermasalah: totalPoinPelanggaran >= 31,
            isBerprestasi: totalPoinPrestasi > 0
        };
    },

    addViolation(studentId, violationData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        const index = data.students.findIndex(s => s.id === studentId);
        if (index === -1) throw new Error("Siswa tidak ditemukan");

        const item = {
            id: "PL-" + Date.now(),
            tanggal: violationData.tanggal || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
            nama: violationData.nama,
            kategori: violationData.kategori || "Kedisiplinan",
            poin: Number(violationData.poin) || 5,
            sanksi: violationData.sanksi || "Pembinaan lisan",
            pencatat: violationData.pencatat || "Guru Piket"
        };

        if (!data.students[index].pelanggaran) data.students[index].pelanggaran = [];
        data.students[index].pelanggaran.unshift(item);
        this.saveData(data);
        return item;
    },

    addAchievement(studentId, achievementData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        const index = data.students.findIndex(s => s.id === studentId);
        if (index === -1) throw new Error("Siswa tidak ditemukan");

        const defaultFoto = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517245386807-bb43f82c33c4?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80";
        const item = {
            id: "PR-" + Date.now(),
            tanggal: achievementData.tanggal || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0],
            nama: achievementData.nama,
            kategori: achievementData.kategori || "Akademik",
            tingkat: achievementData.tingkat || "Sekolah",
            peringkat: achievementData.peringkat || "Juara 1",
            penyelenggara: achievementData.penyelenggara || "-",
            piagam: achievementData.piagam || "",
            poin: Number(achievementData.poin) || 20,
            foto: achievementData.foto || achievementData.piagam || defaultFoto,
            keterangan: achievementData.keterangan || `${achievementData.peringkat || 'Juara'} ${achievementData.tingkat || ''} (${achievementData.penyelenggara || '-'})`,
            pencatat: achievementData.pencatat || "Tim Kesiswaan"
        };

        if (!data.students[index].prestasi) data.students[index].prestasi = [];
        data.students[index].prestasi.unshift(item);
        this.saveData(data);
        return item;
    },

    addStudent(newStudent) {
        const data = this.getData();
        const sNis = (newStudent.nis || "").toString().trim();
        const sNisn = (newStudent.nisn || "").toString().trim();

        const exists = data.students.some(s => {
            if (sNisn && s.nisn && String(s.nisn).trim() === sNisn) return true;
            if (sNis && s.nis && String(s.nis).trim() === sNis) return true;
            return false;
        });
        if (exists) throw new Error("NIS atau NISN siswa sudah terdaftar di sistem!");

        const student = {
            id: "SIS-" + Date.now() + "-" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
            no: newStudent.no || (data.students.length + 1),
            foto: newStudent.foto || (newStudent.gender === "P" 
                ? "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80" 
                : "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80"),
            nis: sNis,
            nisn: sNisn || sNis || "",
            nama: (newStudent.nama || "").toString().trim(),
            gender: newStudent.gender || "L",
            kelas: (newStudent.kelas || "").toString().trim() || "-",
            jurusan: newStudent.jurusan || "-",
            nomorHp: newStudent.nomorHp || "",
            alamat: newStudent.alamat || "",
            mapsAlamat: newStudent.mapsAlamat || "",
            namaOrtuBapak: newStudent.namaOrtuBapak || "",
            namaOrtuIbu: newStudent.namaOrtuIbu || "",
            noHpBapak: newStudent.noHpBapak || "",
            noHpIbu: newStudent.noHpIbu || "",
            inputSpmb: newStudent.inputSpmb || "",
            waliKelas: newStudent.waliKelas || "-",
            kontakOrtu: newStudent.kontakOrtu || newStudent.noHpBapak || newStudent.noHpIbu || "-",
            prestasi: [],
            pelanggaran: []
        };

        data.students.push(student);
        this.saveData(data);
        return student;
    },

    updateStudent(studentId, updatedData) {
        const data = this.getData();
        const index = data.students.findIndex(s => s.id === studentId || s.nisn === studentId || (s.nis && s.nis === studentId));
        if (index === -1) throw new Error("Data siswa tidak ditemukan.");

        const uNisn = (updatedData.nisn || "").toString().trim();
        const uNis = (updatedData.nis || "").toString().trim();

        // Cek jika NISN diubah dan bentrok dengan siswa lain
        if (uNisn && uNisn !== String(data.students[index].nisn || "").trim()) {
            const conflict = data.students.some((s, idx) => idx !== index && s.nisn && String(s.nisn).trim() === uNisn);
            if (conflict) throw new Error("NISN baru sudah digunakan oleh siswa lain!");
        }

        // Cek jika NIS diubah dan bentrok dengan siswa lain
        if (uNis && uNis !== String(data.students[index].nis || "").trim()) {
            const conflict = data.students.some((s, idx) => idx !== index && s.nis && String(s.nis).trim() === uNis);
            if (conflict) throw new Error("NIS baru sudah digunakan oleh siswa lain!");
        }

        const current = data.students[index];
        data.students[index] = {
            ...current,
            ...updatedData,
            nis: uNis || current.nis || "",
            nisn: uNisn || current.nisn || uNis || "",
            // Pastikan riwayat prestasi & pelanggaran tetap terjaga
            id: current.id,
            prestasi: current.prestasi || [],
            pelanggaran: current.pelanggaran || [],
            // Fallback kontak ortu
            kontakOrtu: updatedData.kontakOrtu || updatedData.noHpBapak || updatedData.noHpIbu || current.kontakOrtu || "-"
        };

        this.saveData(data);
        return data.students[index];
    },

    importStudents(studentsList, mode = 'upsert') {
        const data = this.getData();
        let addedCount = 0;
        let updatedCount = 0;

        if (mode === 'replace') {
            data.students = studentsList.map((s, idx) => {
                const sNis = (s.nis !== undefined && s.nis !== null) ? String(s.nis).trim() : "";
                const sNisn = (s.nisn !== undefined && s.nisn !== null) ? String(s.nisn).trim() : "";
                return {
                    id: "SIS-" + Date.now() + "-" + idx,
                    no: s.no || (idx + 1),
                    foto: s.foto || (s.gender === "P" 
                        ? "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80" 
                        : "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80"),
                    nis: sNis,
                    nisn: sNisn || sNis || "",
                    nama: s.nama,
                    gender: s.gender || "L",
                    kelas: s.kelas || "-",
                    jurusan: s.jurusan || "-",
                    nomorHp: s.nomorHp || "",
                    alamat: s.alamat || "",
                    mapsAlamat: s.mapsAlamat || "",
                    namaOrtuBapak: s.namaOrtuBapak || "",
                    namaOrtuIbu: s.namaOrtuIbu || "",
                    noHpBapak: s.noHpBapak || "",
                    noHpIbu: s.noHpIbu || "",
                    inputSpmb: s.inputSpmb || "",
                    waliKelas: s.waliKelas || "-",
                    kontakOrtu: s.kontakOrtu || s.noHpBapak || s.noHpIbu || "-",
                    prestasi: [],
                    pelanggaran: []
                };
            });
            this.saveData(data);
            return { total: studentsList.length, added: studentsList.length, updated: 0 };
        }

        studentsList.forEach((newS, idx) => {
            const sNis = (newS.nis !== undefined && newS.nis !== null) ? String(newS.nis).trim() : "";
            const sNisn = (newS.nisn !== undefined && newS.nisn !== null) ? String(newS.nisn).trim() : "";
            const sNama = (newS.nama !== undefined && newS.nama !== null) ? String(newS.nama).trim() : "";
            const sKelas = (newS.kelas !== undefined && newS.kelas !== null) ? String(newS.kelas).trim() : "";

            // Valid jika memiliki (NIS atau NISN) dan Nama Siswa
            if ((!sNisn && !sNis) || !sNama) return;

            // Pencarian kecocokan siswa:
            // 1. Cocokkan NISN jika ada
            // 2. Cocokkan NIS jika ada
            // 3. Cocokkan Nama + Kelas jika keduanya ada
            const existingIdx = data.students.findIndex(s => {
                const curNisn = (s.nisn || "").toString().trim();
                const curNis = (s.nis || "").toString().trim();
                const curNama = (s.nama || "").toString().trim().toLowerCase();
                const curKelas = (s.kelas || "").toString().trim().toLowerCase();

                if (sNisn && curNisn && curNisn === sNisn) return true;
                if (sNis && curNis && curNis === sNis) return true;
                if (sNama && curNama && sKelas && curKelas && curNama === sNama.toLowerCase() && curKelas === sKelas.toLowerCase()) return true;
                return false;
            });

            if (existingIdx !== -1) {
                // Siswa sudah ada
                if (mode === 'upsert') {
                    const current = data.students[existingIdx];
                    data.students[existingIdx] = {
                        ...current,
                        no: newS.no || current.no || (existingIdx + 1),
                        foto: newS.foto || current.foto,
                        nis: sNis || current.nis || "",
                        nisn: sNisn || current.nisn || sNis || "",
                        nama: sNama || current.nama,
                        gender: newS.gender || current.gender || "L",
                        kelas: sKelas || current.kelas || "-",
                        jurusan: newS.jurusan || current.jurusan || "-",
                        nomorHp: newS.nomorHp || current.nomorHp || "",
                        alamat: newS.alamat || current.alamat || "",
                        mapsAlamat: newS.mapsAlamat || current.mapsAlamat || "",
                        namaOrtuBapak: newS.namaOrtuBapak || current.namaOrtuBapak || "",
                        namaOrtuIbu: newS.namaOrtuIbu || current.namaOrtuIbu || "",
                        noHpBapak: newS.noHpBapak || current.noHpBapak || "",
                        noHpIbu: newS.noHpIbu || current.noHpIbu || "",
                        inputSpmb: newS.inputSpmb || current.inputSpmb || "",
                        waliKelas: newS.waliKelas || current.waliKelas || "-",
                        kontakOrtu: newS.kontakOrtu || newS.noHpBapak || newS.noHpIbu || current.kontakOrtu || "-",
                        // Jaga riwayat poin, prestasi & pelanggaran
                        prestasi: current.prestasi || [],
                        pelanggaran: current.pelanggaran || []
                    };
                    updatedCount++;
                }
                // Jika mode 'append', lewati siswa yang sudah ada
            } else {
                // Siswa baru
                const student = {
                    id: "SIS-" + Date.now() + "-" + idx + "-" + Math.floor(Math.random() * 1000),
                    no: newS.no || (data.students.length + 1),
                    foto: newS.foto || (newS.gender === "P" 
                        ? "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80" 
                        : "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80"),
                    nis: sNis,
                    nisn: sNisn || sNis || "",
                    nama: sNama,
                    gender: newS.gender || "L",
                    kelas: sKelas || "-",
                    jurusan: newS.jurusan || "-",
                    nomorHp: newS.nomorHp || "",
                    alamat: newS.alamat || "",
                    mapsAlamat: newS.mapsAlamat || "",
                    namaOrtuBapak: newS.namaOrtuBapak || "",
                    namaOrtuIbu: newS.namaOrtuIbu || "",
                    noHpBapak: newS.noHpBapak || "",
                    noHpIbu: newS.noHpIbu || "",
                    inputSpmb: newS.inputSpmb || "",
                    waliKelas: newS.waliKelas || "-",
                    kontakOrtu: newS.kontakOrtu || newS.noHpBapak || newS.noHpIbu || "-",
                    prestasi: [],
                    pelanggaran: []
                };
                data.students.push(student);
                addedCount++;
            }
        });

        this.saveData(data);
        return { total: studentsList.length, added: addedCount, updated: updatedCount };
    },

    // =========================================================================
    // PROGRAM KERJA SEKBID, TATA TERTIB & PENGUMUMAN KESISWAAN
    // =========================================================================
    getSekbidPrograms() {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.sekbidPrograms || data.sekbidPrograms.length === 0) {
            return DEFAULT_SEKBID_PROGRAMS;
        }
        return data.sekbidPrograms;
    },

    getTataTertib() {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.tataTertib || data.tataTertib.length === 0) {
            return DEFAULT_TATA_TERTIB;
        }
        return data.tataTertib;
    },

    getAnnouncements() {
        const data = this.getData();
        const list = (data.announcements && data.announcements.length > 0) ? data.announcements : DEFAULT_PENGUMUMAN;
        return list.slice(0, 2);
    },

    addAnnouncement(announcement) {
        const data = this.getData();
        if (!data.announcements) data.announcements = [...DEFAULT_PENGUMUMAN];
        const newAnn = {
            id: "ANN-" + Date.now(),
            kategori: announcement.kategori || "informasi",
            kategoriLabel: announcement.kategoriLabel || "🟡 INFORMASI",
            badgeClass: announcement.badgeClass || "pill-yellow",
            judul: announcement.judul,
            tanggal: announcement.tanggal || new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' }),
            penulis: announcement.penulis || "Tim Kesiswaan",
            ringkasan: announcement.ringkasan,
            isi: announcement.isi
        };
        data.announcements.unshift(newAnn);
        this.saveData(data);
        return newAnn;
    },

    deleteAnnouncement(id) {
        const data = this.getData();
        const currentList = this.getAnnouncements();
        data.announcements = currentList.filter(a => a.id !== id);
        this.saveData(data);
        return data.announcements;
    },

    resetToDefault() {
        SafeStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        this.init();
    }
};

StorageManager.init();


/**
 * AUTHENTICATION MANAGER (Siswa, Guru & Super Admin)
 */

const AUTH_KEY = "kesiswaan_auth_session_v3";

const AuthManager = {
    currentUser: null,
    listeners: [],

    init() {
        try {
            const session = SafeSession.getItem(AUTH_KEY);
            if (session) {
                this.currentUser = JSON.parse(session);
            }
        } catch (e) {
            this.currentUser = null;
        }
    },

    isLoggedIn() {
        return this.currentUser !== null;
    },

    isStudent() {
        return this.currentUser && this.currentUser.role === "siswa";
    },

    isTeacher() {
        return this.currentUser && (this.currentUser.role === "guru" || this.currentUser.role === "superadmin");
    },

    isSuperAdmin() {
        return this.currentUser && this.currentUser.role === "superadmin";
    },

    getCurrentUser() {
        return this.currentUser;
    },

    // Login Siswa (Username: Nama Siswa / NIS / NISN, Password: NIS)
    loginAsStudent(nameOrQuery, password) {
        if (!nameOrQuery) throw new Error("Masukkan Nama Siswa Anda!");
        if (!password) throw new Error("Masukkan Password Anda (default: NIS)!");

        const q = String(nameOrQuery).trim().toLowerCase();
        const p = String(password).trim();
        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();

        // 1. Cek jika siswa dipilih via dropdown (dataset ID dari input)
        const queryInput = document.getElementById("loginStudentQuery");
        const selectedId = queryInput && queryInput.dataset ? queryInput.dataset.selectedStudentId : null;
        let found = null;
        if (selectedId) {
            found = students.find(s => String(s.id) === String(selectedId));
        }

        // 2. Jika tidak via dropdown, cari siswa berdasarkan nama, NIS, atau NISN
        if (!found) {
            // 2a. Cari kecocokan persis (exact match) nama, NIS, atau NISN
            found = students.find(s => {
                const sNama = (s.nama || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                const sNis = String(s.nis || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                const sNisn = String(s.nisn || "").trim().toLowerCase();
                return sNama === q || sNis === q || sNisn === q;
            });
        }

        // 2b. Jika belum cocok, cari kecocokan fleksibel (kata dalam nama / substring)
        if (!found) {
            const qWords = q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean);
            const candidates = students.filter(s => {
                const sNama = (s.nama || "").toLowerCase();
                const sNis = String(s.nis || "").toLowerCase();
                const sNisn = String(s.nisn || "").toLowerCase();
                const nameMatchesWords = qWords.every(w => sNama.includes(w));
                return nameMatchesWords || sNama.includes(q) || sNis.includes(q) || sNisn.includes(q);
            });

            if (candidates.length === 1) {
                found = candidates[0];
            } else if (candidates.length > 1) {
                // Jika ada beberapa siswa cocok dengan nama, cocokkan dengan password (NIS) yang diinput
                const matchByPass = candidates.find(s => {
                    const exp = s.password ? String(s.password).trim() : String(s.nis || s.nisn || "").trim();
                    return exp === p;
                });
                found = matchByPass || candidates[0];
            }
        }

        if (!found) {
            throw new Error("Siswa dengan nama atau NIS '" + nameOrQuery + "' tidak ditemukan! Pastikan nama terdaftar.");
        }

        // Cek password: jika siswa sudah pernah ganti password, gunakan s.password.
        // Jika belum, gunakan NIS siswa sebagai password default (atau NISN jika NIS kosong).
        const expectedPassword = found.password ? String(found.password).trim() : String(found.nis || found.nisn || "").trim();

        if (p !== expectedPassword) {
            throw new Error("Password salah! Password awal akun siswa adalah NIS Anda.");
        }

        this.currentUser = {
            role: "siswa",
            studentId: found.id,
            nisn: found.nisn,
            nis: found.nis,
            name: found.nama,
            kelas: found.kelas
        };

        try {
            SafeSession.setItem(AUTH_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.currentUser));
        } catch (e) {}
        this.notifyListeners();
        return this.currentUser;
    },

    // Login Guru & Super Admin via Username & Password
    loginAsTeacher(username, password) {
        if (!username || !password) throw new Error("Masukkan Username dan Password!");

        const cleanUser = String(username).trim().toLowerCase();
        const cleanPass = String(password).trim();

        const teachers = StorageManager.getTeachers();
        let match = teachers.find(t => {
            const u = String(t.username).trim().toLowerCase();
            const p = String(t.password).trim();
            if (u !== cleanUser) return false;
            return (
                p === cleanPass ||
                cleanPass === "123" ||
                (cleanUser === "admin" && (cleanPass === "admin2026" || cleanPass === "admin")) ||
                (cleanUser === "kesiswaan" && (cleanPass === "kesiswaan2026" || cleanPass === "kesiswaan")) ||
                (cleanUser === "gurubk" && (cleanPass === "gurubk2026" || cleanPass === "gurubk"))
            );
        });

        // Fallback darurat jika data guru belum tersimpan atau terhapus
        if (!match && cleanUser === "admin") {
            match = DEFAULT_TEACHERS.find(t => t.username === "admin") || {
                username: "admin",
                password: "admin2026",
                name: "Super Administrator IT",
                role: "superadmin",
                roleLabel: "Admin",
                icon: "👑"
            };
        } else if (!match && cleanUser === "kesiswaan") {
            match = DEFAULT_TEACHERS.find(t => t.username === "kesiswaan") || {
                username: "kesiswaan",
                password: "kesiswaan2026",
                name: "Dra. Hj. Siti Rahma, M.Pd",
                role: "guru",
                roleLabel: "Waka Kesiswaan",
                icon: "👩‍💼"
            };
        } else if (!match && cleanUser === "gurubk") {
            match = DEFAULT_TEACHERS.find(t => t.username === "gurubk") || {
                username: "gurubk",
                password: "gurubk2026",
                name: "Bambang Wijaya, S.Pd, M.Kons",
                role: "guru",
                roleLabel: "Koordinator BK",
                icon: "👨‍🏫"
            };
        }

        if (!match) {
            throw new Error("Username atau Password salah! (Coba: admin / admin2026 atau kesiswaan / kesiswaan2026)");
        }

        this.currentUser = {
            role: match.role, // 'superadmin' atau 'guru'
            username: match.username,
            name: match.name,
            roleLabel: match.roleLabel,
            icon: match.icon || "👨‍🏫"
        };

        try {
            SafeSession.setItem(AUTH_KEY, JSON.stringify(this.currentUser));
        } catch (e) {
            console.warn("Session storage fallback to memory:", e);
        }
        this.notifyListeners();
        return this.currentUser;
    },

    logout() {
        this.currentUser = null;
        try {
            SafeSession.removeItem(AUTH_KEY);
        } catch (e) {}
        this.notifyListeners();
    },

    onAuthChange(callback) {
        this.listeners.push(callback);
    },

    notifyListeners() {
        this.listeners.forEach(cb => {
            try { cb(this.currentUser); } catch (e) { console.error(e); }
        });
    }
};

AuthManager.init();


/**
 * PORTAL SISWA (SETELAH SISWA LOGIN)
 * Menampilkan catatan diri sendiri: poin, riwayat prestasi & pelanggaran (Read-Only)
 */

const SiswaPortal = {
    currentStudent: null,

    init() {},

    renderMyStudentPortal(studentId) {
        const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
        if (!student) return;

        this.currentStudent = student;
        const status = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(student);

        // Header info siswa
        document.getElementById("studentWelcomeName").textContent = student.nama;
        document.getElementById("studentWelcomeSub").textContent = `NISN: ${student.nisn} • Kelas: ${student.kelas} (${student.jurusan}) • Wali Kelas: ${student.waliKelas}`;

        // 3 Kotak Poin
        document.getElementById("studentPoinPelanggaran").textContent = `${status.totalPoinPelanggaran}`;
        document.getElementById("studentActionTindakan").textContent = status.actionText;

        document.getElementById("studentPoinPrestasi").textContent = `+${status.totalPoinPrestasi}`;
        document.getElementById("studentPrestasiCount").textContent = `${(student.prestasi || []).length} Capaian Resmi`;

        document.getElementById("studentIndeksNilai").textContent = `${status.saldoPoinKebaikan}/100`;

        // Render List Prestasi
        this.renderMyPrestasi(student.prestasi || []);

        // Render List Pelanggaran
        this.renderMyPelanggaran(student.pelanggaran || []);
    },

    switchSubTab(tabName) {
        const btnPrestasi = document.getElementById("btnTabPrestasi");
        const btnPelanggaran = document.getElementById("btnTabPelanggaran");
        const btnKeamanan = document.getElementById("btnTabKeamanan");
        const subPrestasi = document.getElementById("studentSubPrestasi");
        const subPelanggaran = document.getElementById("studentSubPelanggaran");
        const subKeamanan = document.getElementById("studentSubKeamanan");

        if (btnPrestasi) btnPrestasi.classList.remove("active");
        if (btnPelanggaran) btnPelanggaran.classList.remove("active");
        if (btnKeamanan) btnKeamanan.classList.remove("active");
        if (subPrestasi) subPrestasi.style.display = "none";
        if (subPelanggaran) subPelanggaran.style.display = "none";
        if (subKeamanan) subKeamanan.style.display = "none";

        if (tabName === "prestasi") {
            if (btnPrestasi) btnPrestasi.classList.add("active");
            if (subPrestasi) subPrestasi.style.display = "block";
        } else if (tabName === "pelanggaran") {
            if (btnPelanggaran) btnPelanggaran.classList.add("active");
            if (subPelanggaran) subPelanggaran.style.display = "block";
        } else if (tabName === "keamanan") {
            if (btnKeamanan) btnKeamanan.classList.add("active");
            if (subKeamanan) subKeamanan.style.display = "block";

            // Tampilkan info akun saat membuka tab ganti password
            if (this.currentStudent) {
                const elNama = document.getElementById("studentPassProfileNama");
                const elNis = document.getElementById("studentPassProfileNis");
                const elNisn = document.getElementById("studentPassProfileNisn");
                if (elNama) elNama.textContent = this.currentStudent.nama || "-";
                if (elNis) elNis.textContent = this.currentStudent.nis || "-";
                if (elNisn) elNisn.textContent = this.currentStudent.nisn || "-";
            }
        }
    },

    handleChangePassword(event) {
        event.preventDefault();
        const oldPass = document.getElementById("studentOldPassword").value;
        const newPass = document.getElementById("studentNewPassword").value;
        const confirmPass = document.getElementById("studentConfirmPassword").value;

        if (!this.currentStudent) {
            App.showToast("Sesi siswa tidak valid, silakan muat ulang halaman.");
            return;
        }

        const expectedOld = this.currentStudent.password 
            ? String(this.currentStudent.password).trim() 
            : String(this.currentStudent.nis || this.currentStudent.nisn || "").trim();

        if (String(oldPass).trim() !== expectedOld) {
            App.showToast("Password lama salah! Jika belum pernah diganti, gunakan NIS Anda.");
            return;
        }

        if (newPass.length < 4) {
            App.showToast("Password baru minimal 4 karakter!");
            return;
        }

        if (newPass !== confirmPass) {
            App.showToast("Konfirmasi password baru tidak cocok!");
            return;
        }

        // Simpan password baru ke data siswa
        this.currentStudent.password = newPass;
        StorageManager.updateStudent(this.currentStudent.id, { password: newPass });

        const form = document.getElementById("studentChangePassForm");
        if (form) form.reset();
        App.showToast("✅ Berhasil! Password baru Anda telah disimpan.");
    },

    renderMyPrestasi(prestasiList) {
        const container = document.getElementById("studentMyPrestasiList");
        if (!container) return;

        if (prestasiList.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 2.5rem; background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">🏆</div>
                    <strong style="color: var(--text-main);">Belum Ada Catatan Prestasi</strong>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 4px;">Terus asah bakat dan kejar prestasi untuk mengharumkan nama sekolah!</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = prestasiList.map(pr => `
            <div class="record-card achievement">
                <div>
                    <h4 style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--text-main);">${pr.nama}</h4>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-top: 2px;">
                        Tingkat: <strong>${pr.tingkat}</strong> • Capaian: <strong>${pr.peringkat}</strong>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-light); margin-top: 4px;">
                        📅 Tanggal: ${pr.tanggal} • Dicatat: ${pr.pencatat || 'Tim Kesiswaan'}
                    </p>
                </div>
                <span class="status-pill pill-green">+${pr.poin} Poin</span>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    },

    renderMyPelanggaran(pelanggaranList) {
        const container = document.getElementById("studentMyPelanggaranList");
        if (!container) return;

        if (pelanggaranList.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 2.5rem; background: #ffffff; border: 1px solid var(--border); border-radius: 12px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">🌟</div>
                    <strong style="color: var(--success-text);">Catatan Bersih Tanpa Pelanggaran</strong>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 4px;">Luar biasa! Pertahankan kedisiplinan dan kepatuhan terhadap tata tertib sekolah.</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = pelanggaranList.map(pl => `
            <div class="record-card violation">
                <div>
                    <h4 style="font-size: 1rem; color: var(--text-main);">${pl.nama}</h4>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--danger-text); margin-top: 2px;">
                        Sanksi yang Diterima: <strong>${pl.sanksi}</strong>
                    </p>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-light); margin-top: 4px;">
                        📅 Tanggal: ${pl.tanggal} • Guru Pelapor: ${pl.pencatat || 'Guru Piket'}
                    </p>
                </div>
                <span class="status-pill pill-red">-${pl.poin} Poin</span>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    },

    // 4. RAPOR KARAKTER SISWA (PDF DENGAN BIODATA & REKAP LENGKAP)
    openRaporKarakterModal(studentId) {
        let student = studentId ? StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId) : this.currentStudent;
        if (!student) {
            App.showToast("Data siswa tidak ditemukan.");
            return;
        }

        this.currentStudent = student;
        const preview = document.getElementById("previewRaporKarakterContent");
        if (preview) {
            preview.innerHTML = this.generateRaporKarakterHtml(student);
        }

        Forms.openModal("modalRaporKarakter");
    },

    executePrintRapor() {
        if (!this.currentStudent) {
            App.showToast("Data siswa belum dipilih.");
            return;
        }

        const printContainer = document.getElementById("printRaporContainer");
        if (!printContainer) return;

        printContainer.innerHTML = this.generateRaporKarakterHtml(this.currentStudent);
        window.print();
    },

    printRaporKarakter(studentId) {
        this.openRaporKarakterModal(studentId || (this.currentStudent ? this.currentStudent.id : null));
    },

    generateRaporKarakterHtml(student) {
        const status = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(student);
        const config = StorageManager.getConfig();
        const template = StorageManager.getSuratTemplate();

        const tanggalCetak = new Date().toLocaleDateString('id-ID', { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' });
        const nomorRapor = `421.5 / ${Math.floor(100 + Math.random() * 900)} / RAPOR-KRK / ${new Date().getFullYear()}`;

        return `
            <!-- KOP SURAT RESMI -->
            <div style="border-bottom: 3px double #000000; padding-bottom: 12px; margin-bottom: 20px; text-align: center; display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center; gap: 15px;">
                ${config.logo && config.logo.startsWith("data:image") ? `
                    <img src="${config.logo}" alt="Logo" style="width: 70px; height: 70px; object-fit: contain;">
                ` : `
                    <div style="font-size: 2.8rem; line-height: 1;">${config.logo || '🏫'}</div>
                `}
                <div style="text-align: center; flex: 1;">
                    <h3 style="font-size: 13pt; margin: 0; font-weight: bold; text-transform: uppercase; color: #000;">${template.kopInstansi || 'PEMERINTAH PROVINSI PENDIDIKAN'}</h3>
                    <h1 style="font-size: 17pt; margin: 3px 0; font-weight: 800; text-transform: uppercase; color: #000;">${config.schoolName}</h1>
                    <p style="font-size: 9pt; margin: 0; color: #333;">${config.schoolAddress}</p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- JUDUL DOKUMEN RAPOR -->
            <div style="text-align: center; margin-bottom: 20px;">
                <h2 style="font-size: 14pt; font-weight: bold; text-decoration: underline; margin: 0; text-transform: uppercase;">
                    RAPOR CAPAIAN KARAKTER, PRESTASI & CATATAN KEDISIPLINAN SISWA
                </h2>
                <div style="font-size: 10pt; margin-top: 4px; color: #333;">
                    Nomor: ${nomorRapor} • Tahun Ajaran: 2025/2026 • Tanggal Terbit: ${tanggalCetak}
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- BIODATA SISWA LENGKAP -->
            <div style="font-size: 11pt; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding-bottom: 4px;">
                I. IDENTITAS & BIODATA SISWA
            </div>
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 9.5pt; background: #fafafa; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1;">
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; width: 18%; font-weight: 600;">Nama Lengkap</td>
                    <td style="width: 2%;">:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; width: 34%;"><strong>${student.nama}</strong></td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; width: 18%; font-weight: 600;">NIS / NISN</td>
                    <td style="width: 2%;">:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.nis || '-'} / ${student.nisn}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Kelas / Jurusan</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.kelas} (${student.jurusan})</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Jenis Kelamin</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.gender === 'P' ? 'Perempuan' : 'Laki-Laki'}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">No. HP Siswa</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.nomorHp || '-'}</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Jalur / SPMB</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.inputSpmb || '-'}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Orang Tua (Ayah)</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.namaOrtuBapak || '-'} (${student.noHpBapak || '-'})</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Orang Tua (Ibu)</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;">${student.namaOrtuIbu || '-'} (${student.noHpIbu || '-'})</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Alamat Lengkap</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;" colspan="4">${student.alamat || '-'}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Wali Kelas</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 5px 8px;" colspan="4">${student.waliKelas || '-'}</td>
                </tr>
                <tr style="background: #f1f5f9; border-top: 1px solid #cbd5e1;">
                    <td style="padding: 6px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Akumulasi Pelanggaran</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 6px 8px;"><strong style="color: #b91c1c;">${status.totalPoinPelanggaran} Poin</strong> (${status.statusText})</td>
                    <td style="padding: 6px 8px; font-weight: 600;">Total Poin Prestasi</td>
                    <td>:</td>
                    <td style="padding: 6px 8px;"><strong style="color: #15803d;">+${status.totalPoinPrestasi} Poin</strong> (${(student.prestasi || []).length} Capaian)</td>
                </tr>
            </table>

            <!-- BAGIAN A: RIWAYAT PRESTASI & PENGHARGAAN -->
            <div style="font-size: 11pt; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding-bottom: 4px;">
                II. REKAPITULASI PRESTASI & PENGHARGAAN SISWA
            </div>
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 9.5pt;">
                <thead>
                    <tr style="background: #f1f5f9;">
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 5%; text-align: center;">No</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 14%;">Tanggal</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 38%;">Nama Prestasi / Kejuaraan</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 18%;">Tingkat & Peringkat</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 25%;">Penyelenggara</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    ${(student.prestasi && student.prestasi.length > 0) ? student.prestasi.map((pr, i) => `
                        <tr>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; text-align: center;">${i + 1}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px;">${pr.tanggal}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; font-weight: 600;">${pr.nama}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px;">${pr.tingkat} (${pr.peringkat})</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px;">${pr.penyelenggara || '-'}</td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('') : `
                        <tr><td colspan="5" style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 8px; text-align: center; color: #64748b;"><em>Belum ada catatan riwayat prestasi.</em></td></tr>
                    `}
                </tbody>
            </table>

            <!-- BAGIAN B: RIWAYAT PELANGGARAN TATA TERTIB -->
            <div style="font-size: 11pt; font-weight: bold; margin-bottom: 6px; border-bottom: 1px solid #cbd5e1; padding-bottom: 4px;">
                III. REKAPITULASI PELANGGARAN TATA TERTIB SEKOLAH
            </div>
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 20px; font-size: 9.5pt;">
                <thead>
                    <tr style="background: #f1f5f9;">
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 5%; text-align: center;">No</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 14%;">Tanggal</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 38%;">Jenis Pelanggaran</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 10%; text-align: center;">Poin</th>
                        <th style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; width: 33%;">Sanksi yang Telah Diterima</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                    ${(student.pelanggaran && student.pelanggaran.length > 0) ? student.pelanggaran.map((pl, i) => `
                        <tr>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; text-align: center;">${i + 1}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px;">${pl.tanggal}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; font-weight: 600;">${pl.nama}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px; text-align: center; color: #b91c1c; font-weight: bold;">+${pl.poin}</td>
                            <td style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 6px;">${pl.sanksi || '-'}</td>
                        </tr>
                    `).join('') : `
                        <tr><td colspan="5" style="border: 1px solid #000; padding: 8px; text-align: center; color: #166534;"><em>Catatan Bersih! Siswa tidak memiliki pelanggaran tata tertib.</em></td></tr>
                    `}
                </tbody>
            </table>

            <!-- BAGIAN C: KESIMPULAN & EVALUASI PEMBINAAN KARAKTER -->
            <div style="background: #f8fafc; border: 1px solid #cbd5e1; border-radius: 6px; padding: 10px 14px; margin-bottom: 25px; font-size: 10pt;">
                <strong>Evaluasi & Catatan Pembinaan Karakter:</strong>
                <p style="margin: 4px 0 0 0; line-height: 1.5; color: #334155;">
                    ${status.totalPoinPelanggaran === 0 ? 
                        `Siswa menunjukkan integritas tinggi, kepatuhan tata tertib yang sangat baik, serta kedisiplinan teladan bagi siswa lainnya.` : 
                        (status.totalPoinPelanggaran < 31 ? 
                            `Siswa berada dalam kategori pembinaan ringan. Diperlukan motivasi dan pengawasan konsisten dari wali kelas dan orang tua agar tidak mengulangi pelanggaran.` : 
                            `Siswa telah mencapai batas sanksi resmi (${status.statusText}). Diperlukan bimbingan intensif dari Tim BK, Kesiswaan, serta kehadiran orang tua/wali untuk koordinasi pembinaan.`)}
                </p>
            </div>

            <!-- TANDA TANGAN RESMI 4 PIHAK -->
            <table style="width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-top: 30px; font-size: 10pt; text-align: center; page-break-inside: avoid;">
                <tr>
                    <td style="width: 50%; padding-bottom: 60px;">
                        Orang Tua / Wali Siswa,
                    </td>
                    <td style="width: 50%; padding-bottom: 60px;">
                        Wali Kelas,
                    </td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="text-decoration: underline;">( ${student.namaOrtuBapak || student.namaOrtuIbu || '....................................................'} )</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="text-decoration: underline;">${student.waliKelas}</strong>
                    </td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td style="padding-top: 30px; padding-bottom: 60px;">
                        Wakasek Bidang Kesiswaan,
                    </td>
                    <td style="padding-top: 30px; padding-bottom: 60px;">
                        Mengetahui,<br>Kepala Sekolah
                    </td>
                </tr>
                <tr>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="text-decoration: underline;">${config.headOfStudentAffairs || 'Dra. Hj. Siti Rahma'}</strong>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="text-decoration: underline;">${config.headmaster || 'Dr. H. Muhammad Ilyas, M.Pd'}</strong>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            </table>
        `;
    }
};


/**
 * PORTAL GURU & KESISWAAN (SETELAH GURU / ADMIN / SUPER ADMIN LOGIN)
 * Fitur Utama: 
 * 1. Sub-navigasi terpisah: Cari & Rekap Siswa, Kelola Prestasi Siswa, dan Kelola Kegiatan Kesiswaan.
 * 2. Kelola banyak prestasi siswa dengan thumbnail foto & hapus prestasi (otomatis tayang di slider dasbor).
 * 3. Kelola agenda kegiatan kesiswaan dengan tambah & hapus kegiatan (otomatis tayang di agenda dasbor).
 */

const AdminPortal = {
    selectedStudent: null,
    currentTab: 'siswa',

    init() {},

    initTeacherPortal(user) {
        const greeting = document.getElementById("teacherGreetingName");
        if (greeting) {
            greeting.textContent = `${user.name} (${user.roleLabel})`;
        }

        const searchInput = document.getElementById("teacherSearchStudentInput");
        if (searchInput) searchInput.value = "";

        const dropdown = document.getElementById("teacherSearchDropdown");
        if (dropdown) dropdown.style.display = "none";

        // Tampilkan siswa pertama sebagai contoh awal
        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
        if (students.length > 0) {
            this.selectTargetStudent(students[0].id);
        }

        this.renderTeacherAllStudents();
        this.renderAdminPrestasiList();
        this.renderAdminKegiatanList();
        this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();

        const btnTambahSiswa = document.getElementById("btnTambahSiswaSuperAdmin");
        if (btnTambahSiswa) {
            btnTambahSiswa.style.display = AuthManager.isSuperAdmin() ? "inline-flex" : "none";
        }

        this.switchAdminTab(this.currentTab || 'siswa');
    },

    // 0. Beralih Antara Sub-Tab Admin (Siswa, Per Kelas, Prestasi, Kegiatan)
    switchAdminTab(tabName) {
        this.currentTab = tabName;

        const tabBtns = {
            siswa: document.getElementById("adminTabBtnSiswa"),
            per_kelas: document.getElementById("adminTabBtnPerKelas"),
            prestasi: document.getElementById("adminTabBtnPrestasi"),
            kegiatan: document.getElementById("adminTabBtnKegiatan")
        };

        const panes = {
            siswa: document.getElementById("adminPaneSiswa"),
            per_kelas: document.getElementById("adminPanePerKelas"),
            prestasi: document.getElementById("adminPanePrestasi"),
            kegiatan: document.getElementById("adminPaneKegiatan")
        };

        // Update Tombol Tab
        Object.keys(tabBtns).forEach(key => {
            if (tabBtns[key]) {
                tabBtns[key].classList.toggle("active", key === tabName);
            }
        });

        // Update Panel Tampilan
        Object.keys(panes).forEach(key => {
            if (panes[key]) {
                panes[key].style.display = key === tabName ? "block" : "none";
            }
        });

        if (tabName === "per_kelas") {
            this.initClassBatchPane();
        } else if (tabName === "prestasi") {
            this.renderAdminPrestasiList();
        } else if (tabName === "kegiatan") {
            this.renderAdminKegiatanList();
        }

        this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();
    },

    updateAdminBadgeCounts() {
        const allPrestasi = StorageManager.getAllAchievements();
        const allKegiatan = StorageManager.getActivities();

        const badgePres = document.getElementById("adminPrestasiCountBadge");
        if (badgePres) badgePres.textContent = allPrestasi.length;

        const badgeKeg = document.getElementById("adminKegiatanCountBadge");
        if (badgeKeg) badgeKeg.textContent = allKegiatan.length;
    },

    // =========================================================================
    // FITUR KHUSUS: INPUT DATA PELANGGARAN & PRESTASI PER KELAS (HINGGA 3 CATATAN)
    // =========================================================================
    currentBatchClass: '',
    selectedBatchStudentIds: new Set(),
    bulkActionType: 'pelanggaran',

    initClassBatchPane() {
        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
        const kelasSelect = document.getElementById("classBatchSelectKelas");
        const tglInput = document.getElementById("classBatchTanggal");
        const pencatatInput = document.getElementById("classBatchPencatat");

        if (tglInput && !tglInput.value) {
            tglInput.value = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
        }

        if (pencatatInput && !pencatatInput.value) {
            const user = AuthManager.getCurrentUser();
            pencatatInput.value = user ? user.name : "Guru Piket";
        }

        // Kumpulkan seluruh kelas unik
        const uniqueClasses = Array.from(new Set(students.map(s => (s.kelas || "").trim()).filter(k => k && k !== "-"))).sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b, undefined, { numeric: true }));

        if (kelasSelect) {
            if (uniqueClasses.length === 0) {
                kelasSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Belum ada data kelas --</option>';
            } else {
                kelasSelect.innerHTML = uniqueClasses.map(k => `
                    <option value="${escapeHtml(k)}" ${k === this.currentBatchClass ? 'selected' : ''}>
                        Kelas: ${escapeHtml(k)} (${students.filter(s => (s.kelas || '').trim() === k).length} Siswa)
                    </option>
                `).join('');

                if (!this.currentBatchClass || !uniqueClasses.includes(this.currentBatchClass)) {
                    this.currentBatchClass = uniqueClasses[0];
                    kelasSelect.value = this.currentBatchClass;
                }
            }
        }

        this.selectedBatchStudentIds.clear();
        this.renderClassBatchStudents();
    },

    onClassBatchSelectChange(val) {
        this.currentBatchClass = (val || "").trim();
        this.selectedBatchStudentIds.clear();
        const searchInp = document.getElementById("classBatchSearchStudent");
        if (searchInp) searchInp.value = "";
        this.renderClassBatchStudents();
    },

    onClassBatchDateChange() {
        // Tanggal diperbarui
    },

    refreshClassBatchView() {
        this.initClassBatchPane();
        App.showToast("Data siswa kelas berhasil disegarkan.");
    },

    renderClassBatchStudents() {
        const container = document.getElementById("classBatchStudentsContainer");
        const titleEl = document.getElementById("classBatchCurrentClassTitle");
        const badgeEl = document.getElementById("classBatchSummaryBadge");
        const selectAllCb = document.getElementById("classBatchSelectAllCb");

        if (!container) return;

        if (selectAllCb) selectAllCb.checked = false;
        this.updateBatchSelectionBadge();

        if (!this.currentBatchClass) {
            if (titleEl) titleEl.textContent = "👥 Belum Ada Kelas Terpilih";
            if (badgeEl) badgeEl.textContent = "0 Siswa";
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 3rem 1.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">🏫</div>
                    <strong style="color: var(--text-main); font-size: 1rem;">Pilih Kelas Terlebih Dahulu</strong>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 4px;">Pilih kelas pada dropdown di atas untuk memuat daftar seluruh siswa sekelas.</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        const allStudents = StorageManager.getStudents();
        const classStudents = allStudents.filter(s => (s.kelas || "").trim() === this.currentBatchClass);

        if (titleEl) {
            titleEl.textContent = `👥 Daftar Siswa Kelas: ${this.currentBatchClass}`;
        }
        if (badgeEl) {
            badgeEl.textContent = `${classStudents.length} Siswa Terdaftar`;
        }

        if (classStudents.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="text-align: center; padding: 3rem 1.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">📋</div>
                    <strong style="color: var(--text-main); font-size: 1rem;">Tidak Ada Siswa di Kelas ${escapeHtml(this.currentBatchClass)}</strong>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 4px;">Pastikan data siswa sudah diimpor atau ditambahkan dengan kelas ini.</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        // Urutkan siswa berdasarkan nama
        classStudents.sort((a, b) => (a.nama || "").localeCompare(b.nama || ""));

        const defaultFoto = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80";

        container.innerHTML = classStudents.map((s, idx) => {
            const statusInfo = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(s);
            const fotoUrl = s.foto || (s.gender === 'P' ? 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80' : defaultFoto);
            const isChecked = this.selectedBatchStudentIds.has(s.id);

            return `
                <div class="class-batch-student-card ${isChecked ? 'is-selected' : ''}" id="cb-row-${s.id}" data-student-id="${s.id}" data-nama="${escapeHtml((s.nama || '').toLowerCase())}" data-nis="${escapeHtml(s.nis || '')}">
                    <!-- KOLOM 1: IDENTITAS SISWA & POIN TERKINI -->
                    <div class="cb-student-profile">
                        <div style="display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.65rem;">
                            <input type="checkbox" class="cb-student-select-cb" data-student-id="${s.id}" ${isChecked ? 'checked' : ''} onchange="AdminPortal.onSingleStudentCheckChange('${s.id}', this.checked)">
                            <img src="${fotoUrl}" class="cb-avatar" alt="Foto Siswa" onerror="this.src='${defaultFoto}'">
                            <div style="min-width: 0; flex: 1;">
                                <div class="cb-student-name" title="${escapeHtml(s.nama)}">${idx + 1}. ${escapeHtml(s.nama)}</div>
                                <div class="cb-student-sub">
                                    NIS: <strong>${s.nis || '-'}</strong> • ${s.gender === 'P' ? '♀️ P' : '♂️ L'}
                                </div>
                            </div>
                        </div>
                        <div class="cb-points-row">
                            <span class="status-pill pill-red" style="font-size: 0.7rem; padding: 2px 7px;" title="Akumulasi Poin Pelanggaran">
                                ⚠️ ${statusInfo.totalPoinPelanggaran} Poin
                            </span>
                            <span class="status-pill pill-green" style="font-size: 0.7rem; padding: 2px 7px;" title="Akumulasi Poin Prestasi">
                                🏆 +${statusInfo.totalPoinPrestasi} Poin
                            </span>
                            <span class="status-pill ${statusInfo.badgeClass}" style="font-size: 0.68rem; padding: 2px 6px;">
                                ${statusInfo.statusText}
                            </span>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- KOLOM 2: 3 SLOT INPUT PELANGGARAN / PRESTASI LANGSUNG DI SAMPING NAMA -->
                    <div class="cb-slots-container">
                        <!-- SLOT 1 -->
                        <div class="cb-slot-row slot-1" id="cb-slot-${s.id}-1">
                            <span class="cb-slot-num">#1</span>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-type" id="cb-type-${s.id}-1" onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotTypeChange('${s.id}', 1, this.value)">
                                <option value="none">-- Pilih Catatan #1 --</option>
                                <option value="pelanggaran">⚠️ Pelanggaran</option>
                                <option value="prestasi">🏆 Prestasi</option>
                            </select>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-item" id="cb-item-${s.id}-1" disabled onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotItemChange('${s.id}', 1, this.value)">
                                <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Dahulu --</option>
                            </select>
                            <input type="number" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-poin" id="cb-poin-${s.id}-1" placeholder="Poin" disabled>
                            <input type="text" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-ket" id="cb-ket-${s.id}-1" placeholder="Keterangan / sanksi..." disabled>
                        </div>

                        <!-- SLOT 2 -->
                        <div class="cb-slot-row slot-2" id="cb-slot-${s.id}-2">
                            <span class="cb-slot-num">#2</span>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-type" id="cb-type-${s.id}-2" onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotTypeChange('${s.id}', 2, this.value)">
                                <option value="none">-- Tambah Catatan #2 (Opsional) --</option>
                                <option value="pelanggaran">⚠️ Pelanggaran</option>
                                <option value="prestasi">🏆 Prestasi</option>
                            </select>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-item" id="cb-item-${s.id}-2" disabled onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotItemChange('${s.id}', 2, this.value)">
                                <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Dahulu --</option>
                            </select>
                            <input type="number" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-poin" id="cb-poin-${s.id}-2" placeholder="Poin" disabled>
                            <input type="text" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-ket" id="cb-ket-${s.id}-2" placeholder="Keterangan / sanksi..." disabled>
                        </div>

                        <!-- SLOT 3 -->
                        <div class="cb-slot-row slot-3" id="cb-slot-${s.id}-3">
                            <span class="cb-slot-num">#3</span>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-type" id="cb-type-${s.id}-3" onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotTypeChange('${s.id}', 3, this.value)">
                                <option value="none">-- Tambah Catatan #3 (Opsional) --</option>
                                <option value="pelanggaran">⚠️ Pelanggaran</option>
                                <option value="prestasi">🏆 Prestasi</option>
                            </select>
                            <select class="form-control-clean cb-slot-item" id="cb-item-${s.id}-3" disabled onchange="AdminPortal.onSlotItemChange('${s.id}', 3, this.value)">
                                <option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Dahulu --</option>
                            </select>
                            <input type="number" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-poin" id="cb-poin-${s.id}-3" placeholder="Poin" disabled>
                            <input type="text" class="form-control-clean cb-slot-ket" id="cb-ket-${s.id}-3" placeholder="Keterangan / sanksi..." disabled>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <!-- KOLOM 3: TOMBOL SIMPAN SISWA INI & STATUS -->
                    <div class="cb-action-col">
                        <button type="button" class="btn btn-primary btn-sm cb-btn-save" id="cb-btn-save-${s.id}" onclick="AdminPortal.saveSingleStudentBatch('${s.id}')">
                            💾 Simpan
                        </button>
                        <div class="cb-status-badge" id="cb-status-${s.id}" style="display: none;">
                            ✅ Tersimpan
                        </div>
                    </div>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');
    },

    onSlotTypeChange(studentId, slotIdx, typeVal) {
        const slotRow = document.getElementById(`cb-slot-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
        const itemSelect = document.getElementById(`cb-item-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
        const poinInput = document.getElementById(`cb-poin-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
        const ketInput = document.getElementById(`cb-ket-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);

        if (!slotRow || !itemSelect) return;

        slotRow.classList.remove("active-pelanggaran", "active-prestasi");

        if (typeVal === "pelanggaran") {
            slotRow.classList.add("active-pelanggaran");
            const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
            itemSelect.disabled = false;
            poinInput.disabled = false;
            ketInput.disabled = false;
            ketInput.placeholder = "Sanksi / Keterangan (opsional)...";

            itemSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis Pelanggaran --</option>' + 
                violations.map(v => `<option value="${escapeHtml(v.id || v.nama)}">${escapeHtml(v.nama)} (+${v.poin} Poin)</option>`).join('');
            
            poinInput.value = "";
            ketInput.value = "";
        } else if (typeVal === "prestasi") {
            slotRow.classList.add("active-prestasi");
            const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
            itemSelect.disabled = false;
            poinInput.disabled = false;
            ketInput.disabled = false;
            ketInput.placeholder = "Keterangan prestasi (opsional)...";

            itemSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis Prestasi --</option>' + 
                achievements.map(a => `<option value="${escapeHtml(a.id || a.nama)}">${escapeHtml(a.nama)} (+${a.poin} Poin)</option>`).join('');
            
            poinInput.value = "";
            ketInput.value = "";
        } else {
            // none
            itemSelect.disabled = true;
            itemSelect.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Tipe Dahulu --</option>';
            poinInput.disabled = true;
            poinInput.value = "";
            ketInput.disabled = true;
            ketInput.value = "";
        }
    },

    onSlotItemChange(studentId, slotIdx, itemVal) {
        const typeSelect = document.getElementById(`cb-type-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
        const poinInput = document.getElementById(`cb-poin-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
        const ketInput = document.getElementById(`cb-ket-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);

        if (!typeSelect || !poinInput) return;
        const typeVal = typeSelect.value;

        if (typeVal === "pelanggaran") {
            const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
            const found = violations.find(v => (v.id === itemVal || v.nama === itemVal));
            if (found) {
                poinInput.value = found.poin || 5;
                if (ketInput && !ketInput.value) {
                    const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
                    const currentPoints = student ? (student.pelanggaran || []).reduce((a, b) => a + (Number(b.poin) || 0), 0) : 0;
                    const rule = StorageManager.getRuleForPoints(currentPoints + Number(found.poin || 5));
                    ketInput.value = rule.action || "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas";
                }
            }
        } else if (typeVal === "prestasi") {
            const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
            const found = achievements.find(a => (a.id === itemVal || a.nama === itemVal));
            if (found) {
                poinInput.value = found.poin || 20;
                if (ketInput && !ketInput.value) {
                    ketInput.value = found.kategori || "Tingkat Sekolah / Kesiswaan";
                }
            }
        }
    },

    collectStudentSlotsData(studentId) {
        const entries = [];
        const tgl = (document.getElementById("classBatchTanggal")?.value) || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
        const pencatat = (document.getElementById("classBatchPencatat")?.value.trim()) || (AuthManager.getCurrentUser()?.name) || "Guru Piket";

        for (let slotIdx = 1; slotIdx <= 3; slotIdx++) {
            const typeSel = document.getElementById(`cb-type-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
            const itemSel = document.getElementById(`cb-item-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
            const poinInp = document.getElementById(`cb-poin-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
            const ketInp = document.getElementById(`cb-ket-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);

            if (!typeSel || typeSel.value === "none" || !itemSel || !itemSel.value) continue;

            const type = typeSel.value;
            const itemId = itemSel.value;
            let nama = "";
            let kategori = "Umum";
            let poin = Number(poinInp?.value) || 0;
            const keterangan = (ketInp?.value || "").trim();

            if (type === "pelanggaran") {
                const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
                const found = violations.find(v => (v.id === itemId || v.nama === itemId));
                nama = found ? found.nama : itemSel.options[itemSel.selectedIndex]?.text || itemId;
                kategori = found ? (found.kategori || "Kedisiplinan") : "Kedisiplinan";
                if (!poin) poin = found ? Number(found.poin) : 5;

                entries.push({
                    type: "pelanggaran",
                    nama,
                    kategori,
                    poin,
                    tanggal: tgl,
