                    sanksi: keterangan || "Pembinaan lisan & wali kelas",
                    pencatat
                });
            } else if (type === "prestasi") {
                const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
                const found = achievements.find(a => (a.id === itemId || a.nama === itemId));
                nama = found ? found.nama : itemSel.options[itemSel.selectedIndex]?.text || itemId;
                kategori = found ? (found.kategori || "Akademik") : "Akademik";
                if (!poin) poin = found ? Number(found.poin) : 20;

                entries.push({
                    type: "prestasi",
                    nama,
                    kategori,
                    tingkat: "Sekolah",
                    peringkat: "Penghargaan/Juara",
                    penyelenggara: "Kesiswaan / Sekolah",
                    poin,
                    tanggal: tgl,
                    keterangan: keterangan || nama,
                    pencatat
                });
            }
        }

        return entries;
    },

    saveSingleStudentBatch(studentId) {
        const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
        if (!student) {
            App.showToast("Data siswa tidak ditemukan.");
            return;
        }

        const entries = this.collectStudentSlotsData(studentId);
        if (entries.length === 0) {
            App.showToast(`Pilih minimal 1 pelanggaran atau prestasi untuk siswa ${student.nama} terlebih dahulu.`);
            return;
        }

        try {
            entries.forEach(entry => {
                if (entry.type === "pelanggaran") {
                    StorageManager.addViolation(studentId, {
                        nama: entry.nama,
                        kategori: entry.kategori,
                        poin: entry.poin,
                        tanggal: entry.tanggal,
                        sanksi: entry.sanksi,
                        pencatat: entry.pencatat
                    });
                } else if (entry.type === "prestasi") {
                    StorageManager.addAchievement(studentId, {
                        nama: entry.nama,
                        kategori: entry.kategori,
                        tingkat: entry.tingkat,
                        peringkat: entry.peringkat,
                        penyelenggara: entry.penyelenggara,
                        poin: entry.poin,
                        tanggal: entry.tanggal,
                        keterangan: entry.keterangan,
                        pencatat: entry.pencatat
                    });
                }
            });

            // Update badge poin & status di baris siswa tersebut secara instan
            const updatedStudent = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
            const statusInfo = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(updatedStudent);
            const rowEl = document.getElementById(`cb-row-${studentId}`);
            if (rowEl) {
                const pointsRow = rowEl.querySelector(".cb-points-row");
                if (pointsRow) {
                    pointsRow.innerHTML = `
                        <span class="status-pill pill-red" style="font-size: 0.7rem; padding: 2px 7px;" title="Akumulasi Poin Pelanggaran">
                            ⚠️ ${statusInfo.totalPoinPelanggaran} Poin
                        </span>
                        <span class="status-pill pill-green" style="font-size: 0.7rem; padding: 2px 7px;" title="Akumulasi Poin Prestasi">
                            🏆 +${statusInfo.totalPoinPrestasi} Poin
                        </span>
                        <span class="status-pill ${statusInfo.badgeClass}" style="font-size: 0.68rem; padding: 2px 6px;">
                            ${statusInfo.statusText}
                        </span>
                    `;
                }

                rowEl.classList.add("is-saved");

                // Reset 3 slot ke 'none'
                for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
                    const typeSel = document.getElementById(`cb-type-${studentId}-${i}`);
                    if (typeSel) {
                        typeSel.value = "none";
                        this.onSlotTypeChange(studentId, i, "none");
                    }
                }

                // Tampilkan badge status tersimpan
                const statusBadge = document.getElementById(`cb-status-${studentId}`);
                if (statusBadge) {
                    statusBadge.style.display = "block";
                    statusBadge.textContent = `✅ ${entries.length} Catatan Tersimpan`;
                    setTimeout(() => {
                        if (statusBadge) statusBadge.style.display = "none";
                    }, 4000);
                }
            }

            this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();
            this.renderTeacherAllStudents();

            App.showToast(`✅ Berhasil menyimpan ${entries.length} catatan untuk ${student.nama}!`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message || "Gagal menyimpan data.");
        }
    },

    saveAllClassBatch() {
        const rows = document.querySelectorAll(".class-batch-student-card");
        if (!rows || rows.length === 0) {
            App.showToast("Tidak ada siswa yang ditampilkan.");
            return;
        }

        let totalRecords = 0;
        let studentsCount = 0;
        const tasks = [];

        rows.forEach(row => {
            const studentId = row.getAttribute("data-student-id");
            if (!studentId) return;

            const entries = this.collectStudentSlotsData(studentId);
            if (entries.length > 0) {
                tasks.push({ studentId, entries });
                totalRecords += entries.length;
                studentsCount++;
            }
        });

        if (tasks.length === 0) {
            App.showToast("Belum ada data pelanggaran atau prestasi yang diisi pada baris siswa manapun.");
            return;
        }

        try {
            tasks.forEach(task => {
                task.entries.forEach(entry => {
                    if (entry.type === "pelanggaran") {
                        StorageManager.addViolation(task.studentId, {
                            nama: entry.nama,
                            kategori: entry.kategori,
                            poin: entry.poin,
                            tanggal: entry.tanggal,
                            sanksi: entry.sanksi,
                            pencatat: entry.pencatat
                        });
                    } else if (entry.type === "prestasi") {
                        StorageManager.addAchievement(task.studentId, {
                            nama: entry.nama,
                            kategori: entry.kategori,
                            tingkat: entry.tingkat,
                            peringkat: entry.peringkat,
                            penyelenggara: entry.penyelenggara,
                            poin: entry.poin,
                            tanggal: entry.tanggal,
                            keterangan: entry.keterangan,
                            pencatat: entry.pencatat
                        });
                    }
                });
            });

            this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();
            this.renderTeacherAllStudents();
            this.renderClassBatchStudents();

            App.showToast(`🎉 Sukses! Berhasil menyimpan total ${totalRecords} catatan untuk ${studentsCount} siswa di kelas ${this.currentBatchClass}!`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message || "Gagal menyimpan data massal.");
        }
    },

    toggleSelectAllClassBatch(isChecked) {
        const cbs = document.querySelectorAll(".cb-student-select-cb");
        cbs.forEach(cb => {
            const parentRow = cb.closest(".class-batch-student-card");
            if (parentRow && parentRow.style.display !== "none") {
                cb.checked = isChecked;
                const studentId = cb.getAttribute("data-student-id");
                if (studentId) {
                    if (isChecked) {
                        this.selectedBatchStudentIds.add(studentId);
                        parentRow.classList.add("is-selected");
                    } else {
                        this.selectedBatchStudentIds.delete(studentId);
                        parentRow.classList.remove("is-selected");
                    }
                }
            }
        });
        this.updateBatchSelectionBadge();
    },

    onSingleStudentCheckChange(studentId, isChecked) {
        if (isChecked) {
            this.selectedBatchStudentIds.add(studentId);
        } else {
            this.selectedBatchStudentIds.delete(studentId);
        }
        const row = document.getElementById(`cb-row-${studentId}`);
        if (row) {
            row.classList.toggle("is-selected", isChecked);
        }
        this.updateBatchSelectionBadge();
    },

    updateBatchSelectionBadge() {
        const badge = document.getElementById("classBatchSelectedCountBadge");
        if (badge) {
            badge.textContent = `${this.selectedBatchStudentIds.size} siswa dipilih`;
            badge.className = this.selectedBatchStudentIds.size > 0 ? "status-pill pill-blue" : "status-pill pill-gray";
        }
    },

    filterClassBatchRows(query) {
        const q = (query || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        const rows = document.querySelectorAll(".class-batch-student-card");
        rows.forEach(row => {
            const nama = row.getAttribute("data-nama") || "";
            const nis = row.getAttribute("data-nis") || "";
            if (!q || nama.includes(q) || nis.includes(q)) {
                row.style.display = "grid";
            } else {
                row.style.display = "none";
            }
        });
    },

    clearAllClassBatchInputs() {
        const rows = document.querySelectorAll(".class-batch-student-card");
        rows.forEach(row => {
            const studentId = row.getAttribute("data-student-id");
            if (studentId) {
                for (let i = 1; i <= 3; i++) {
                    const typeSel = document.getElementById(`cb-type-${studentId}-${i}`);
                    if (typeSel) {
                        typeSel.value = "none";
                        this.onSlotTypeChange(studentId, i, "none");
                    }
                }
            }
            row.classList.remove("is-saved");
        });
        App.showToast("Semua isian formulir kelas telah dibersihkan.");
    },

    // MODAL TERAPKAN MASSAL KE SISWA TERCENTANG
    openBulkQuickModal(type) {
        if (this.selectedBatchStudentIds.size === 0) {
            App.showToast("Centang minimal 1 siswa di tabel terlebih dahulu.");
            return;
        }

        this.bulkActionType = type;
        const titleEl = document.getElementById("modalBulkClassTitle");
        const noticeEl = document.getElementById("modalBulkClassNoticeText");
        const labelEl = document.getElementById("modalBulkItemLabel");
        const selectEl = document.getElementById("modalBulkItemSelect");
        const poinInp = document.getElementById("modalBulkPoin");
        const ketInp = document.getElementById("modalBulkKeterangan");

        if (titleEl) {
            titleEl.textContent = type === "pelanggaran" ? "⚠️ Terapkan Pelanggaran Massal ke Siswa Terpilih" : "🏆 Terapkan Prestasi Massal ke Siswa Terpilih";
        }

        if (noticeEl) {
            noticeEl.innerHTML = `Terapkan ke <strong>${this.selectedBatchStudentIds.size} siswa</strong> yang sedang dicentang di kelas ${escapeHtml(this.currentBatchClass)}.`;
        }

        if (labelEl) {
            labelEl.textContent = type === "pelanggaran" ? "Pilih Jenis Pelanggaran: *" : "Pilih Jenis Prestasi: *";
        }

        if (selectEl) {
            if (type === "pelanggaran") {
                const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
                selectEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis Pelanggaran --</option>' + 
                    violations.map(v => `<option value="${escapeHtml(v.id || v.nama)}">${escapeHtml(v.nama)} (+${v.poin} Poin)</option>`).join('');
                if (violations.length > 0) {
                    selectEl.value = violations[0].id || violations[0].nama;
                    if (poinInp) poinInp.value = violations[0].poin;
                    if (ketInp) ketInp.value = "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas";
                }
            } else {
                const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
                selectEl.innerHTML = '<option value="">-- Pilih Jenis Prestasi --</option>' + 
                    achievements.map(a => `<option value="${escapeHtml(a.id || a.nama)}">${escapeHtml(a.nama)} (+${a.poin} Poin)</option>`).join('');
                if (achievements.length > 0) {
                    selectEl.value = achievements[0].id || achievements[0].nama;
                    if (poinInp) poinInp.value = achievements[0].poin;
                    if (ketInp) ketInp.value = achievements[0].kategori || "Tingkat Sekolah";
                }
            }
        }

        Forms.openModal("modalBulkClassAction");
    },

    onBulkModalItemChange(val) {
        const poinInp = document.getElementById("modalBulkPoin");
        const ketInp = document.getElementById("modalBulkKeterangan");

        if (this.bulkActionType === "pelanggaran") {
            const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
            const found = violations.find(v => (v.id === val || v.nama === val));
            if (found) {
                if (poinInp) poinInp.value = found.poin || 5;
                if (ketInp) ketInp.value = "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas";
            }
        } else {
            const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
            const found = achievements.find(a => (a.id === val || a.nama === val));
            if (found) {
                if (poinInp) poinInp.value = found.poin || 20;
                if (ketInp) ketInp.value = found.kategori || "Tingkat Sekolah";
            }
        }
    },

    executeBulkClassAction() {
        const itemSel = document.getElementById("modalBulkItemSelect");
        const slotIdx = document.getElementById("modalBulkSlotIndex")?.value || "1";
        const poin = Number(document.getElementById("modalBulkPoin")?.value) || 0;
        const ket = (document.getElementById("modalBulkKeterangan")?.value || "").trim();
        const autoSave = document.getElementById("modalBulkAutoSaveCb")?.checked;

        if (!itemSel || !itemSel.value) {
            App.showToast("Pilih jenis terlebih dahulu.");
            return;
        }

        const selectedIds = Array.from(this.selectedBatchStudentIds);
        if (selectedIds.length === 0) {
            App.showToast("Tidak ada siswa terpilih.");
            return;
        }

        const type = this.bulkActionType;
        const itemId = itemSel.value;
        let nama = "";
        let kategori = "Umum";

        if (type === "pelanggaran") {
            const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
            const found = violations.find(v => (v.id === itemId || v.nama === itemId));
            nama = found ? found.nama : itemSel.options[itemSel.selectedIndex]?.text || itemId;
            kategori = found ? (found.kategori || "Kedisiplinan") : "Kedisiplinan";
        } else {
            const achievements = StorageManager.getAchievementsMaster();
            const found = achievements.find(a => (a.id === itemId || a.nama === itemId));
            nama = found ? found.nama : itemSel.options[itemSel.selectedIndex]?.text || itemId;
            kategori = found ? (found.kategori || "Akademik") : "Akademik";
        }

        const tgl = (document.getElementById("classBatchTanggal")?.value) || new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
        const pencatat = (document.getElementById("classBatchPencatat")?.value.trim()) || (AuthManager.getCurrentUser()?.name) || "Guru Piket";

        if (autoSave) {
            try {
                selectedIds.forEach(studentId => {
                    if (type === "pelanggaran") {
                        StorageManager.addViolation(studentId, {
                            nama,
                            kategori,
                            poin,
                            tanggal: tgl,
                            sanksi: ket || "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas",
                            pencatat
                        });
                    } else {
                        StorageManager.addAchievement(studentId, {
                            nama,
                            kategori,
                            tingkat: "Sekolah",
                            peringkat: "Penghargaan",
                            penyelenggara: "Kesiswaan / Sekolah",
                            poin,
                            tanggal: tgl,
                            keterangan: ket || nama,
                            pencatat
                        });
                    }
                });

                Forms.closeAllModals();
                this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();
                this.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                this.renderClassBatchStudents();

                App.showToast(`⚡ Berhasil menyimpan ${type === 'pelanggaran' ? 'pelanggaran' : 'prestasi'} untuk ${selectedIds.length} siswa terpilih!`);
            } catch (err) {
                App.showToast(err.message || "Gagal menerapkan secara massal.");
            }
        } else {
            // Hanya isi slot formulir agar bisa ditinjau guru
            selectedIds.forEach(studentId => {
                const typeSel = document.getElementById(`cb-type-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
                if (typeSel) {
                    typeSel.value = type;
                    this.onSlotTypeChange(studentId, slotIdx, type);
                    const itemEl = document.getElementById(`cb-item-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
                    if (itemEl) itemEl.value = itemId;
                    const poinEl = document.getElementById(`cb-poin-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
                    if (poinEl) poinEl.value = poin;
                    const ketEl = document.getElementById(`cb-ket-${studentId}-${slotIdx}`);
                    if (ketEl) ketEl.value = ket;
                }
            });

            Forms.closeAllModals();
            App.showToast(`Slot #${slotIdx} untuk ${selectedIds.length} siswa telah terisi. Klik "Simpan Semua" jika sudah sesuai.`);
        }
    },

    // 1. Live Search Siswa Saat Guru Mengetik
    handleTeacherSearch(query) {
        const dropdown = document.getElementById("teacherSearchDropdown");
        if (!dropdown) return;

        const q = (query || "").trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!q) {
            dropdown.style.display = "none";
            return;
        }

        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
        const matches = students.filter(s => 
            (s.nama && s.nama.toLowerCase().includes(q)) || 
            (s.nis && String(s.nis).toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
            (s.nisn && String(s.nisn).toLowerCase().includes(q)) ||
            (s.kelas && s.kelas.toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );

        if (matches.length === 0) {
            dropdown.innerHTML = `<div style="padding: 0.75rem 1rem; color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 0.85rem;">Tidak ada siswa yang cocok.</div>`;
            dropdown.style.display = "block";
            return;
        }

        dropdown.innerHTML = matches.map(s => {
            const st = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(s);
            const idInfo = s.nis ? `NIS: ${s.nis}${s.nisn && s.nisn !== s.nis ? ' • NISN: ' + s.nisn : ''}` : `NISN: ${s.nisn || '-'}`;
            return `
                <div style="padding: 0.65rem 1rem; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--border); cursor: pointer; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center;"
                     onclick="AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent('${s.id}')"
                     onmouseover="this.style.background='#f1f5f9'"
                     onmouseout="this.style.background='#ffffff'">
                    <div>
                        <strong style="color: var(--text-main); font-size: 0.9rem;">${s.nama}</strong>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted);">${s.kelas} • ${idInfo}</div>
                    </div>
                    <span class="status-pill ${st.badgeClass}" style="font-size: 0.7rem;">${st.statusText}</span>
                </div>
            `;
        }).join('');

        dropdown.style.display = "block";
    },

    submitTeacherSearch() {
        const input = document.getElementById("teacherSearchStudentInput");
        if (!input) return;
        const q = input.value.trim().toLowerCase();
        if (!q) {
            App.showToast("Ketik nama, NIS, atau NISN siswa terlebih dahulu");
            return;
        }

        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
        const match = students.find(s => 
            (s.nama && s.nama.toLowerCase().includes(q)) || 
            (s.nis && String(s.nis).toLowerCase().includes(q)) || 
            (s.nisn && String(s.nisn).toLowerCase().includes(q))
        );
        if (match) {
            this.selectTargetStudent(match.id);
        } else {
            App.showToast("Siswa tidak ditemukan.");
        }
    },

    // 2. Pilih Siswa yang Dituju (Menampilkan Kartu Tindakan Siswa)
    selectTargetStudent(studentId) {
        const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
        if (!student) return;

        this.selectedStudent = student;
        const st = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(student);

        // Tutup dropdown search jika terbuka
        const dropdown = document.getElementById("teacherSearchDropdown");
        if (dropdown) dropdown.style.display = "none";

        const input = document.getElementById("teacherSearchStudentInput");
        if (input) input.value = student.nama;

        // Isi data ke kartu tindakan siswa
        document.getElementById("targetStudentFoto").src = student.foto;
        document.getElementById("targetStudentNama").textContent = student.nama;
        const idBadge = student.nis ? `NIS: ${student.nis}${student.nisn && student.nisn !== student.nis ? ' • NISN: ' + student.nisn : ''}` : `NISN: ${student.nisn || '-'}`;
        document.getElementById("targetStudentInfo").textContent = `${idBadge} • Kelas: ${student.kelas} (${student.jurusan || '-'})`;
        document.getElementById("targetStudentWali").textContent = student.waliKelas || "-";

        // Rincian Lengkap Profil Format Excel Impor
        const elNis = document.getElementById("targetStudentNis");
        if (elNis) elNis.textContent = student.nis || "-";

        const elNisn = document.getElementById("targetStudentNisn");
        if (elNisn) elNisn.textContent = student.nisn || "-";

        const elHp = document.getElementById("targetStudentHp");
        if (elHp) elHp.textContent = student.nomorHp || "-";

        const elSpmb = document.getElementById("targetStudentSpmb");
        if (elSpmb) elSpmb.textContent = student.inputSpmb || "-";

        const elAyah = document.getElementById("targetStudentAyah");
        if (elAyah) elAyah.textContent = student.namaOrtuBapak || "-";

        const elHpBapak = document.getElementById("targetStudentHpBapak");
        if (elHpBapak) elHpBapak.textContent = student.noHpBapak || "-";

        const elIbu = document.getElementById("targetStudentIbu");
        if (elIbu) elIbu.textContent = student.namaOrtuIbu || "-";

        const elHpIbu = document.getElementById("targetStudentHpIbu");
        if (elHpIbu) elHpIbu.textContent = student.noHpIbu || "-";

        const elAlamat = document.getElementById("targetStudentAlamat");
        if (elAlamat) elAlamat.textContent = student.alamat || "-";

        const mapsBtn = document.getElementById("targetStudentMapsLink");
        if (mapsBtn) {
            if (student.mapsAlamat && student.mapsAlamat.trim()) {
                mapsBtn.href = student.mapsAlamat;
                mapsBtn.style.display = "inline-flex";
            } else if (student.alamat && student.alamat.trim() && student.alamat !== "-") {
                mapsBtn.href = "https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=" + encodeURIComponent(student.alamat);
                mapsBtn.style.display = "inline-flex";
            } else {
                mapsBtn.style.display = "none";
            }
        }

        // Status & Poin
        const pill = document.getElementById("targetStudentStatusPill");
        if (pill) {
            pill.className = `status-pill ${st.badgeClass}`;
            pill.textContent = st.statusText;
        }

        document.getElementById("targetStudentPoinPelanggaran").textContent = `${st.totalPoinPelanggaran} Poin`;
        document.getElementById("targetStudentPoinPrestasi").textContent = `+${st.totalPoinPrestasi} Poin`;

        // KONTROL OTOMATIS SURAT PANGGILAN ORANG TUA:
        // HANYA MUNCUL JIKA SUDAH MELEWATI BATAS POIN KADAR SP (>= 31 Poin)
        const isEligibleSp = StorageManager.isStudentEligibleForSummons(student);
        const btnPanggil = document.getElementById("btnPanggilOrtuTarget");
        const noticeBelumSp = document.getElementById("noticeBelumSpTarget");

        if (btnPanggil) {
            if (isEligibleSp) {
                btnPanggil.style.display = "inline-flex";
                btnPanggil.innerHTML = `📄 Cetak Surat Panggilan Ortu (${st.statusText})`;
            } else {
                btnPanggil.style.display = "none";
            }
        }

        if (noticeBelumSp) {
            noticeBelumSp.style.display = isEligibleSp ? "none" : "inline-flex";
        }

        const card = document.getElementById("targetStudentCard");
        if (card) {
            card.style.display = "block";
        }

        App.showToast(`Siswa terpilih: ${student.nama}`);
    },

    // 3. Tombol-Tombol Aksi pada Siswa yang Dituju
    actionAddViolation() {
        if (!this.selectedStudent) {
            const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
            if (students.length > 0) this.selectTargetStudent(students[0].id);
        }
        if (!this.selectedStudent) return;
        Forms.openViolationModal(this.selectedStudent.id);
    },

    actionAddAchievement() {
        if (!this.selectedStudent) {
            const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
            if (students.length > 0) this.selectTargetStudent(students[0].id);
        }
        if (!this.selectedStudent) return;
        Forms.openAchievementModal(this.selectedStudent.id);
    },

    actionPrintSummons() {
        if (!this.selectedStudent) {
            App.showToast("Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu.");
            return;
        }
        if (!StorageManager.isStudentEligibleForSummons(this.selectedStudent)) {
            App.showToast("Siswa belum mencapai batas poin SP untuk penerbitan surat panggilan orang tua.");
            return;
        }
        Forms.openSuratPanggilanModal(this.selectedStudent.id);
    },

    actionPrintReport() {
        if (!this.selectedStudent) {
            App.showToast("Pilih siswa terlebih dahulu.");
            return;
        }
        SiswaPortal.openRaporKarakterModal(this.selectedStudent.id);
    },

    topStudentsSort: 'prestasi', // 'prestasi' | 'pelanggaran' | 'max'

    setTopStudentsSort(mode) {
        this.topStudentsSort = mode;
        const btnPrestasi = document.getElementById("btnSortPrestasi");
        const btnPelanggaran = document.getElementById("btnSortPelanggaran");
        const btnAll = document.getElementById("btnSortAll");
        const title = document.getElementById("topStudentsTitle");

        if (btnPrestasi) btnPrestasi.classList.toggle("active", mode === 'prestasi');
        if (btnPelanggaran) btnPelanggaran.classList.toggle("active", mode === 'pelanggaran');
        if (btnAll) btnAll.classList.toggle("active", mode === 'max');

        if (title) {
            if (mode === 'prestasi') {
                title.textContent = "🏆 10 Siswa Teratas (Poin Prestasi Tertinggi)";
            } else if (mode === 'pelanggaran') {
                title.textContent = "⚠️ 10 Siswa Teratas (Poin Pelanggaran Tertinggi)";
            } else {
                title.textContent = "🌟 10 Siswa Teratas (Poin Tertinggi)";
            }
        }

        this.renderTeacherAllStudents();
    },

    // 4. Render Tabel 10 Siswa Teratas (Poin Tertinggi) di Halaman Guru
    renderTeacherAllStudents() {
        const tbody = document.getElementById("teacherAllStudentsTableBody");
        if (!tbody) return;

        const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
        const mode = this.topStudentsSort || 'prestasi';

        if (!students || students.length === 0) {
            tbody.innerHTML = `
                <tr>
                    <td colspan="6" style="text-align: center; padding: 2.5rem; color: var(--text-muted);">
                        <div style="font-size: 2rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">📋</div>
                        <strong style="color: var(--text-main);">Belum Ada Data Siswa Terdaftar</strong>
                        <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 4px;">Tambahkan siswa baru atau impor data Excel melalui menu Super Admin.</p>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `;
            return;
        }

        // Hitung status dan score untuk pengurutan
        const studentsWithStatus = students.map(s => {
            const st = StorageManager.calculateStudentStatus(s);
            let score = 0;
            if (mode === 'prestasi') {
                score = st.totalPoinPrestasi || 0;
            } else if (mode === 'pelanggaran') {
                score = st.totalPoinPelanggaran || 0;
            } else {
                score = Math.max(st.totalPoinPrestasi || 0, st.totalPoinPelanggaran || 0);
            }
            return {
                student: s,
                status: st,
                score: score
            };
        });

        // Urutkan siswa berdasarkan score tertinggi secara menurun (descending)
        studentsWithStatus.sort((a, b) => {
            if (b.score !== a.score) {
                return b.score - a.score;
            }
            // Jika skor prestasi sama, prioritaskan yang pelanggarannya lebih sedikit
            if (mode === 'prestasi') {
                if (a.status.totalPoinPelanggaran !== b.status.totalPoinPelanggaran) {
                    return a.status.totalPoinPelanggaran - b.status.totalPoinPelanggaran;
                }
            }
            return a.student.nama.localeCompare(b.student.nama);
        });

        // Cukup tampilkan 10 siswa teratas
        const top10 = studentsWithStatus.slice(0, 10);

        tbody.innerHTML = top10.map((item, idx) => {
            const s = item.student;
            const st = item.status;
            const isSelected = this.selectedStudent && this.selectedStudent.id === s.id;
            const isSp = StorageManager.isStudentEligibleForSummons(s);

            let rankBadge = '';
            if (idx === 0) {
                rankBadge = `<span class="rank-badge rank-1" title="Peringkat 1">🥇 1</span>`;
            } else if (idx === 1) {
                rankBadge = `<span class="rank-badge rank-2" title="Peringkat 2">🥈 2</span>`;
            } else if (idx === 2) {
                rankBadge = `<span class="rank-badge rank-3" title="Peringkat 3">🥉 3</span>`;
            } else {
                rankBadge = `<span class="rank-badge rank-other">${idx + 1}</span>`;
            }

            return `
                <tr style="${isSelected ? 'background: #eff6ff; font-weight: 600;' : ''}">
                    <td style="text-align: center;">${rankBadge}</td>
                    <td>
                        <div style="font-weight: 700; color: var(--text-main); font-size: 0.95rem;">${s.nama}</div>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted);">NISN: ${s.nisn} ${s.nis ? '• NIS: ' + s.nis : ''}</div>
                    </td>
                    <td><span class="status-pill pill-gray">${s.kelas}</span></td>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="color: ${st.totalPoinPelanggaran > 0 ? 'var(--danger)' : 'var(--text-muted)'};">
                            ${st.totalPoinPelanggaran} Poin
                        </strong>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-muted);">${(s.pelanggaran || []).length} Kasus</div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <strong style="color: ${st.totalPoinPrestasi > 0 ? 'var(--success)' : 'var(--text-muted)'};">
                            +${st.totalPoinPrestasi} Poin
                        </strong>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.7rem; color: var(--text-muted);">${(s.prestasi || []).length} Prestasi</div>
                    </td>
                    <td>
                        <div style="display: flex; gap: 5px; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                            <button class="btn ${isSelected ? 'btn-primary' : 'btn-outline'} btn-sm" onclick="AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent('${s.id}')">
                                ${isSelected ? '✓ Terpilih' : '👉 Pilih'}
                            </button>
                            <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="Forms.openViolationModal('${s.id}')" title="Catat Pelanggaran Siswa Ini">
                                ⚠️ Pelanggaran
                            </button>
                            <button class="btn btn-success btn-sm" onclick="Forms.openAchievementModal('${s.id}')" title="Catat Prestasi Siswa Ini">
                                🏆 Prestasi
                            </button>
                            ${isSp ? `
                                <button class="btn btn-warning btn-sm" onclick="Forms.openSuratPanggilanModal('${s.id}')" title="Cetak Surat Panggilan SP (${st.statusText})">
                                    📄 Surat SP
                                </button>
                            ` : ''}
                            <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" onclick="Forms.openEditStudentModal('${s.id}')" title="Edit Profil Siswa Lengkap">
                                ✏️ Edit
                            </button>
                        </div>
                    </td>
                </tr>
            `;
        }).join('');
    },

    // 5. KELOLA PRESTASI SISWA (TAMPILAN TERPISAH DENGAN FOTO & HAPUS)
    renderAdminPrestasiList() {
        const container = document.getElementById("adminPrestasiListContainer");
        if (!container) return;

        const achievements = StorageManager.getAllAchievements();
        this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();

        if (achievements.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="grid-column: 1 / -1; padding: 2.5rem; text-align: center; background: #ffffff; border: 1px dashed var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-md); color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">🏆</div>
                    <h4>Belum Ada Data Prestasi Siswa</h4>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 0.25rem;">Klik tombol <strong>"+ Catat Prestasi Baru"</strong> di atas untuk menambahkan prestasi siswa beserta foto kejuaraan.</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = achievements.map(p => `
            <div class="prestasi-admin-card">
                <div class="prestasi-admin-img-wrap">
                    <img src="${p.foto || 'https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1517245386807-bb43f82c33c4?auto=format&fit=crop&w=800&q=80'}" class="prestasi-admin-img" alt="${p.nama}">
                    <span class="prestasi-admin-badge-float">🏆 ${p.peringkat || 'Juara'}</span>
                </div>
                <div class="prestasi-admin-content">
                    <div>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--primary); font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 2px;">
                            ⭐ ${p.siswaNama} (${p.siswaKelas})
                        </div>
                        <h4 style="font-size: 1.05rem; margin-bottom: 0.35rem; color: var(--text-main);">${p.nama}</h4>
                        <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted); line-height: 1.4;">
                            ${p.penyelenggara && p.penyelenggara !== '-' ? `<div style="color: #475569; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 2px;">🏛️ Penyelenggara: ${p.penyelenggara}</div>` : ''}
                            <div>${p.keterangan || '-'}</div>
                        </div>
                    </div>

                    <div style="margin-top: 1rem; padding-top: 0.75rem; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.5rem;">
                        <div style="display: flex; gap: 0.35rem; align-items: center; flex-wrap: wrap;">
                            <span class="status-pill pill-green">+${p.poin} Poin</span>
                            <span class="status-pill pill-gray" style="font-size: 0.7rem;">${p.tingkat}</span>
                            <span style="font-size: 0.75rem; color: var(--text-muted);">📅 ${p.tanggal}</span>
                            ${p.piagam ? `<a href="${p.piagam}" target="_blank" class="status-pill pill-yellow" style="font-size: 0.7rem; text-decoration: none; font-weight: 600;">📜 Piagam</a>` : ''}
                        </div>
                        <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color: var(--danger); border-color: var(--danger-border);" onclick="AdminPortal.handleDeleteAchievement('${p.id}', '${p.nama}')">
                            🗑️ Hapus
                        </button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    },

    handleDeleteAchievement(achievementId, namaPrestasi) {
        if (confirm(`Yakin ingin menghapus prestasi "${namaPrestasi}"? Data akan otomatis terhapus dari galeri slider dasbor awal.`)) {
            const success = StorageManager.deleteAchievement(achievementId);
            if (success) {
                this.renderAdminPrestasiList();
                this.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                App.initPrestasiSlider();
                App.showToast(`Prestasi "${namaPrestasi}" berhasil dihapus.`);
            } else {
                App.showToast("Gagal menghapus data prestasi.");
            }
        }
    },

    // 6. KELOLA KEGIATAN KESISWAAN (TAMPILAN TERPISAH DENGAN TAMBAH & HAPUS)
    renderAdminKegiatanList() {
        const container = document.getElementById("adminKegiatanListContainer");
        if (!container) return;

        const activities = StorageManager.getActivities();
        this.updateAdminBadgeCounts();

        if (activities.length === 0) {
            container.innerHTML = `
                <div style="grid-column: 1 / -1; padding: 2.5rem; text-align: center; background: #ffffff; border: 1px dashed var(--border); border-radius: var(--radius-md); color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <div style="font-size: 2.5rem; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">📅</div>
                    <h4>Belum Ada Agenda Kegiatan Kesiswaan</h4>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; margin-top: 0.25rem;">Klik tombol <strong>"+ Tambah Agenda Kegiatan"</strong> di atas untuk menambahkan agenda kegiatan sekolah.</p>
                </div>
            `;
            return;
        }

        container.innerHTML = activities.map(act => `
            <div class="kegiatan-admin-card">
                <div>
                    <div style="display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; margin-bottom: 0.5rem; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.25rem;">
                        <span class="kegiatan-date-badge">📅 ${act.tanggal}</span>
                        <span class="status-pill pill-gray" style="font-size: 0.75rem;">${act.kategori || 'Kegiatan Sekolah'}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h4 style="font-size: 1.05rem; margin-bottom: 0.35rem; color: var(--text-main);">${act.nama}</h4>
                    <div style="font-size: 0.8rem; color: var(--text-muted); margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">
                        📍 Lokasi: <strong>${act.tempat || 'Lingkungan Sekolah'}</strong>
                    </div>
                    <p style="font-size: 0.85rem; color: var(--text-main); line-height: 1.4;">${act.keterangan || '-'}</p>
                </div>

                <div style="margin-top: 1rem; padding-top: 0.75rem; border-top: 1px solid var(--border); display: flex; justify-content: flex-end;">
                    <button class="btn btn-outline btn-sm" style="color: var(--danger); border-color: var(--danger-border);" onclick="AdminPortal.handleDeleteActivity('${act.id}', '${act.nama}')">
                        🗑️ Hapus Kegiatan
                    </button>
                </div>
            </div>
        `).join('');
    },

    handleDeleteActivity(activityId, namaKegiatan) {
        if (confirm(`Yakin ingin menghapus agenda kegiatan "${namaKegiatan}"? Kegiatan akan dihapus dari dasbor awal.`)) {
            const success = StorageManager.deleteActivity(activityId);
            if (success) {
                this.renderAdminKegiatanList();
                App.renderDashboardKegiatan();
                App.showToast(`Agenda "${namaKegiatan}" berhasil dihapus.`);
            } else {
                App.showToast("Gagal menghapus kegiatan.");
            }
        }
    }
};


/**
 * CONTROLLER FORMULIR & MODAL
 * Mendukung input foto prestasi, pengaturan sekolah oleh Super Admin, kelola anggota guru, dan kata mutiara.
 */

const Forms = {
    uploadedLogoUrl: null,
    uploadedDashboardBgUrl: null,
    uploadedPrestasiFotoUrl: null,
    uploadedPrestasiPiagamUrl: null,

    init() {
        this.bindEvents();
        this.populateDropdowns();
    },

    resizeImageFile(file, maxWidth, maxHeight, quality, callback) {
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (e) => {
            const img = new Image();
            img.onload = () => {
                const canvas = document.createElement("canvas");
                let width = img.width;
                let height = img.height;

                if (width > maxWidth || height > maxHeight) {
                    if (width / height > maxWidth / maxHeight) {
                        height = Math.round((height * maxWidth) / width);
                        width = maxWidth;
                    } else {
                        width = Math.round((width * maxHeight) / height);
                        height = maxHeight;
                    }
                }

                canvas.width = width;
                canvas.height = height;
                const ctx = canvas.getContext("2d");
                ctx.drawImage(img, 0, 0, width, height);

                const isPng = file.type === "image/png" || file.type === "image/svg+xml";
                const dataUrl = canvas.toDataURL(isPng ? "image/png" : "image/jpeg", quality || 0.82);
                callback(dataUrl);
            };
            img.onerror = () => {
                App.showToast("Gagal memproses file gambar.");
            };
            img.src = e.target.result;
        };
        reader.onerror = () => {
            App.showToast("Gagal membaca file gambar.");
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    },

    clearLogoUpload() {
        this.uploadedLogoUrl = "";
        const box = document.getElementById("logoPreviewBox");
        if (box) box.style.display = "none";
        const fileInput = document.getElementById("setSchoolLogoFile");
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = "";
        this.updateSettingsLivePreview();
    },

    clearDashboardBgUpload() {
        this.uploadedDashboardBgUrl = "";
        const box = document.getElementById("bgPreviewBox");
        if (box) box.style.display = "none";
        const fileInput = document.getElementById("setDashboardBgFile");
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = "";
    },

    clearPrestasiPiagamUpload() {
        this.uploadedPrestasiPiagamUrl = "";
        const box = document.getElementById("prestasiPiagamPreviewBox");
        if (box) box.style.display = "none";
        const fileInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPiagamFile");
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = "";
        const urlInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPiagamUrl");
        if (urlInput) urlInput.value = "";
    },

    clearPrestasiFotoUpload() {
        this.uploadedPrestasiFotoUrl = "";
        const box = document.getElementById("prestasiFotoPreviewBox");
        if (box) box.style.display = "none";
        const fileInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiFotoFile");
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = "";
        const urlInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiFoto");
        if (urlInput) urlInput.value = "";
    },

    updatePrestasiPointsCalculation() {
        const juara = document.getElementById("formPrestasiJuara")?.value || "";
        const tingkat = document.getElementById("formPrestasiTingkat")?.value || "";

        let base = 30;
        const t = tingkat.toLowerCase();
        const j = juara.toLowerCase();

        if (t.includes("internasional")) base = 120;
        else if (t.includes("nasional")) base = 90;
        else if (t.includes("provinsi") || t.includes("jateng") || t.includes("diy")) base = 60;
        else if (t.includes("karesidenan")) base = 45;
        else if (t.includes("kota") || t.includes("kabupaten")) base = 30;

        let bonus = 0;
        if (j === "juara 1") bonus = 15;
        else if (j === "juara 2") bonus = 10;
        else if (j === "juara 3") bonus = 5;
        else if (j.includes("harapan 1")) bonus = 0;
        else if (j.includes("harapan 2")) bonus = -5;
        else if (j.includes("harapan 3")) bonus = -10;

        const totalPoints = Math.max(10, base + bonus);

        const badge = document.getElementById("prestasiPoinBadge");
        if (badge) badge.textContent = `+${totalPoints} Poin`;

        const hiddenInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPoin");
        if (hiddenInput) hiddenInput.value = totalPoints;
    },

    bindEvents() {
        // Submit Pelanggaran
        const vForm = document.getElementById("violationForm");
        if (vForm) {
            vForm.addEventListener("submit", (e) => this.handleViolationSubmit(e));
        }

        // Submit Prestasi
        const aForm = document.getElementById("achievementForm");
        if (aForm) {
            aForm.addEventListener("submit", (e) => this.handleAchievementSubmit(e));
        }

        // Submit Siswa Baru
        const sForm = document.getElementById("studentForm");
        if (sForm) {
            sForm.addEventListener("submit", (e) => this.handleStudentSubmit(e));
        }

        // Upload Logo Sekolah
        const logoFileInput = document.getElementById("setSchoolLogoFile");
        if (logoFileInput) {
            logoFileInput.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
                const file = e.target.files[0];
                if (file) {
                    this.resizeImageFile(file, 220, 220, 0.85, (base64) => {
                        this.uploadedLogoUrl = base64;
                        const preview = document.getElementById("logoPreviewImg");
                        const box = document.getElementById("logoPreviewBox");
                        if (preview && box) {
                            preview.src = base64;
                            box.style.display = "flex";
                        }
                        this.updateSettingsLivePreview();
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // Upload Wallpaper Dasbor
        const bgFileInput = document.getElementById("setDashboardBgFile");
        if (bgFileInput) {
            bgFileInput.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
                const file = e.target.files[0];
                if (file) {
                    this.resizeImageFile(file, 1600, 1000, 0.75, (base64) => {
                        this.uploadedDashboardBgUrl = base64;
                        const preview = document.getElementById("bgPreviewImg");
                        const box = document.getElementById("bgPreviewBox");
                        if (preview && box) {
                            preview.src = base64;
                            box.style.display = "flex";
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // Upload Piagam / Sertifikat Kejuaraan
        const piagamFileInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPiagamFile");
        if (piagamFileInput) {
            piagamFileInput.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
                const file = e.target.files[0];
                if (file) {
                    this.resizeImageFile(file, 1000, 800, 0.82, (base64) => {
                        this.uploadedPrestasiPiagamUrl = base64;
                        const preview = document.getElementById("prestasiPiagamPreviewImg");
                        const box = document.getElementById("prestasiPiagamPreviewBox");
                        if (preview && box) {
                            preview.src = base64;
                            box.style.display = "flex";
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // Upload Foto Dokumentasi Prestasi (Tidak Wajib)
        const presFileInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiFotoFile");
        if (presFileInput) {
            presFileInput.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
                const file = e.target.files[0];
                if (file) {
                    this.resizeImageFile(file, 900, 600, 0.80, (base64) => {
                        this.uploadedPrestasiFotoUrl = base64;
                        const preview = document.getElementById("prestasiFotoPreviewImg");
                        const box = document.getElementById("prestasiFotoPreviewBox");
                        if (preview && box) {
                            preview.src = base64;
                            box.style.display = "flex";
                        }
                    });
                }
            });
        }

        // Kalkulasi Poin Reward saat Peringkat atau Tingkat dipilih
        const presJuara = document.getElementById("formPrestasiJuara");
        if (presJuara) {
            presJuara.addEventListener("change", () => this.updatePrestasiPointsCalculation());
        }

        const presTingkat = document.getElementById("formPrestasiTingkat");
        if (presTingkat) {
            presTingkat.addEventListener("change", () => this.updatePrestasiPointsCalculation());
        }

        // Dropdown Katalog Pelanggaran Auto-fill & Kalkulasi Rekap Poin
        const vCat = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKatalog");
        if (vCat) {
            vCat.addEventListener("change", (e) => {
                const id = e.target.value;
                const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
                const item = violations.find(p => p.id === id);
                const sId = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSiswaId").value;
                const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(sId);

                if (item) {
                    document.getElementById("formPelanggaranNama").value = item.nama;
                    document.getElementById("formPelanggaranPoin").value = item.poin;
                    document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKategori").value = item.kategori || "Kedisiplinan";
                    this.updateViolationCalculation(student, item);
                } else {
                    document.getElementById("formPelanggaranNama").value = "";
                    document.getElementById("formPelanggaranPoin").value = 0;
                    this.updateViolationCalculation(student, null);
                }
            });
        }

        // Klik luar modal untuk menutup
        document.querySelectorAll(".modal-backdrop").forEach(backdrop => {
            backdrop.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
                if (e.target === backdrop) this.closeAllModals();
            });
        });
    },

    populateDropdowns() {
        // Dropdown Katalog Pelanggaran Resmi (Kelolaan Super Admin, Poin Langsung Muncul)
        const pelKatalog = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKatalog");
        if (pelKatalog) {
            const violations = StorageManager.getViolationsMaster();
            pelKatalog.innerHTML = `
                <option value="">-- Pilih Jenis Pelanggaran --</option>
                ${violations.map(p => `
                    <option value="${p.id}">[${p.poin} Poin] ${p.nama} (${p.kategori || 'Kedisiplinan'})</option>
                `).join('')}
            `;
        }
    },

    openModal(modalId) {
        this.closeAllModals();
        const m = document.getElementById(modalId);
        if (m) {
            m.style.display = "";
            m.classList.add("show");
            document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";
        }
    },

    closeModal(modalId) {
        if (modalId) {
            const m = document.getElementById(modalId);
            if (m) {
                m.classList.remove("show");
                m.style.display = "";
            }
            if (modalId === "modalEditTeacher") {
                this.openManageTeachersModal();
                return;
            }
            const anyOpen = document.querySelector(".modal-backdrop.show");
            if (!anyOpen) {
                document.body.style.overflow = "auto";
            }
        } else {
            this.closeAllModals();
        }
    },

    closeAllModals() {
        document.querySelectorAll(".modal-backdrop").forEach(m => {
            m.classList.remove("show");
            m.style.display = "";
        });
        document.body.style.overflow = "auto";
    },

    // Rekapitulasi Otomatis Poin, Riwayat Pelanggaran & Sanksi Terkunci
    updateViolationCalculation(student, violationItem) {
        if (!student) {
            const sId = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSiswaId")?.value;
            student = StorageManager.getStudentById(sId);
        }

        const poinLama = student ? (student.pelanggaran || []).reduce((acc, curr) => acc + (Number(curr.poin) || 0), 0) : 0;
        const poinBaru = violationItem ? Number(violationItem.poin) : 0;
        const poinTotal = poinLama + poinBaru;

        // Update indikator angka di kotak rekap
        const elLama = document.getElementById("rekapPoinLama");
        const elBaru = document.getElementById("rekapPoinBaru");
        const elTotal = document.getElementById("rekapPoinTotal");
        const elPill = document.getElementById("rekapPointCalculationPill");

        if (elLama) elLama.textContent = `${poinLama} Poin`;
        if (elBaru) elBaru.textContent = `+${poinBaru} Poin`;
        if (elTotal) elTotal.textContent = `${poinTotal} Poin`;
        if (elPill) elPill.textContent = `${poinLama} + ${poinBaru} = ${poinTotal} Poin`;

        // Render riwayat pelanggaran sebelumnya
        const riwayatContainer = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranRiwayatList");
        if (riwayatContainer) {
            if (student && student.pelanggaran && student.pelanggaran.length > 0) {
                riwayatContainer.innerHTML = student.pelanggaran.map((p) => `
                    <div style="padding: 4px 0; border-bottom: 1px dotted #fca5a5; display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: center; gap: 8px;">
                        <div style="overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap;">
                            <span style="color: #64748b; font-size: 0.7rem;">${p.tanggal || '-'}</span> 
                            <strong style="color: #b91c1c;">${p.nama}</strong> 
                            <span class="status-pill pill-red" style="font-size: 0.65rem; padding: 1px 6px;">+${p.poin} Poin</span>
                        </div>
                        <span style="color: #7f1d1d; font-size: 0.7rem; font-style: italic; white-space: nowrap;">
                            ${p.sanksi || '-'}
                        </span>
                    </div>
                `).join('');
            } else {
                riwayatContainer.innerHTML = `
                    <div style="color: #16a34a; font-weight: 500; padding: 3px 0;">
                        ✨ Siswa belum memiliki catatan pelanggaran sebelumnya (Catatan Bersih).
                    </div>
                `;
            }
        }

        // Cari sanksi & status otomatis berdasarkan rentangan poin dari aturan resmi
        const rule = StorageManager.getRuleForPoints(poinTotal);
        const sanksiInput = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSanksi");
        if (sanksiInput) {
            sanksiInput.value = rule.action || rule.tindakLanjut || "Peringatan lisan & pembinaan wali kelas";
        }

        const badge = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranStatusBadge");
        if (badge) {
            badge.textContent = rule.status;
            badge.className = `status-pill ${rule.badgeClass || 'pill-green'}`;
        }
    },

    openViolationModal(preselectedStudentId = null) {
        let student = null;
        if (preselectedStudentId) {
            student = StorageManager.getStudentById(preselectedStudentId);
        }
        if (!student && AdminPortal.selectedStudent) {
            student = AdminPortal.selectedStudent;
        }
        if (!student) {
            const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
            if (students && students.length > 0) {
                student = students[0];
            }
        }

        if (!student) {
            App.showToast("Belum ada data siswa.");
            return;
        }

        // Kunci siswa yang dituju (tanpa dropdown lain)
        AdminPortal.selectedStudent = student;
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSiswaId").value = student.id;
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranLockedNama").textContent = student.nama;
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranLockedInfo").textContent = `NISN: ${student.nisn} • Kelas: ${student.kelas} (${student.jurusan || '-'})`;

        // Tanggal kejadian tepat di bawah nama siswa
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranTanggal").value = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];

        // Reset pilihan katalog jenis pelanggaran
        this.populateDropdowns();
        const vCat = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKatalog");
        if (vCat) vCat.value = "";
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranNama").value = "";
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranPoin").value = 0;
        document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKategori").value = "Kedisiplinan";

        // Inisialisasi rekap poin & riwayat awal
        this.updateViolationCalculation(student, null);

        this.openModal("modalViolation");
    },

    openAchievementModal(preselectedStudentId = null) {
        let student = null;
        if (preselectedStudentId) {
            student = StorageManager.getStudentById(preselectedStudentId);
        }
        if (!student && AdminPortal.selectedStudent) {
            student = AdminPortal.selectedStudent;
        }
        if (!student) {
            const students = StorageManager.getStudents();
            if (students && students.length > 0) {
                student = students[0];
            }
        }

        if (!student) {
            App.showToast("Belum ada data siswa.");
            return;
        }

        // Kunci siswa yang dituju (tanpa dropdown lain)
        AdminPortal.selectedStudent = student;
        document.getElementById("formPrestasiSiswaId").value = student.id;
        document.getElementById("formPrestasiLockedNama").textContent = student.nama;
        document.getElementById("formPrestasiLockedInfo").textContent = `NISN: ${student.nisn} • Kelas: ${student.kelas} (${student.jurusan || '-'})`;

        const form = document.getElementById("achievementForm");
        if (form) form.reset();
        this.clearPrestasiFotoUpload();
        this.clearPrestasiPiagamUpload();

        document.getElementById("formPrestasiTanggal").value = new Date().toISOString().split("T")[0];
        const juaraSelect = document.getElementById("formPrestasiJuara");
        if (juaraSelect) juaraSelect.value = "";
        const tingkatSelect = document.getElementById("formPrestasiTingkat");
        if (tingkatSelect) tingkatSelect.value = "";
        this.updatePrestasiPointsCalculation();

        this.openModal("modalAchievement");
    },

    studentFormFormat: 'xi_xii',

    openStudentModal() {
        if (!AuthManager.isSuperAdmin()) {
            App.showToast("Fitur tambah siswa baru hanya dapat diakses oleh Super Admin!");
            return;
        }
        const form = document.getElementById("studentForm");
        if (form) form.reset();
        this.addStudentCurrentFoto = "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80";
        const preview = document.getElementById("formAddStudentFotoPreview");
        if (preview) preview.src = this.addStudentCurrentFoto;
        this.setStudentFormFormat('xi_xii');
        this.openModal("modalStudent");
    },

    setStudentFormFormat(format) {
        this.studentFormFormat = format;
        const btnXiXii = document.getElementById("btnStudentFormFmtXiXii");
        const btnX = document.getElementById("btnStudentFormFmtX");
        const notice = document.getElementById("studentFormXiXiiNotice");
        const extraDetails = document.getElementById("studentFormExtraDetails");
        const nisLabel = document.getElementById("formStudentNisLabel");
        const nisInput = document.getElementById("formStudentNis");
        const nisnLabel = document.getElementById("formStudentNisnLabel");
        const nisnInput = document.getElementById("formStudentNisn");

        if (format === 'xi_xii') {
            if (btnXiXii) { btnXiXii.className = "btn btn-primary btn-sm"; }
            if (btnX) { btnX.className = "btn btn-outline btn-sm"; }
            if (notice) notice.style.display = "flex";
            if (extraDetails) extraDetails.open = false;
            if (nisLabel) nisLabel.innerHTML = 'Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS) <strong style="color:var(--danger)">*</strong>';
            if (nisInput) nisInput.required = true;
            if (nisnLabel) nisnLabel.innerHTML = 'Nomor NISN (Opsional)';
            if (nisnInput) nisnInput.required = false;
        } else {
            if (btnXiXii) { btnXiXii.className = "btn btn-outline btn-sm"; }
            if (btnX) { btnX.className = "btn btn-primary btn-sm"; }
            if (notice) notice.style.display = "none";
            if (extraDetails) extraDetails.open = true;
            if (nisLabel) nisLabel.innerHTML = 'Nomor Induk Siswa (NIS)';
            if (nisInput) nisInput.required = false;
            if (nisnLabel) nisnLabel.innerHTML = 'Nomor NISN <strong style="color:var(--danger)">*</strong>';
            if (nisnInput) nisnInput.required = true;
        }
    },

    handleStudentFormKelasInput(kelasVal) {
        const val = (kelasVal || '').trim().toUpperCase();
        if (val.startsWith('XI ') || val.startsWith('XII ') || val === 'XI' || val === 'XII') {
            if (this.studentFormFormat !== 'xi_xii') {
                this.setStudentFormFormat('xi_xii');
            }
        } else if (val.startsWith('X ') || val === 'X') {
            if (this.studentFormFormat !== 'x') {
                this.setStudentFormFormat('x');
            }
        }
    },

    // 1. Submit Pelanggaran
    handleViolationSubmit(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const studentId = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSiswaId").value;
        const nama = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranNama").value;
        const poin = Number(document.getElementById("formPelanggaranPoin").value);
        const tanggal = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranTanggal").value;
        const sanksi = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranSanksi").value;
        const kategori = document.getElementById("formPelanggaranKategori").value || "Kedisiplinan";

        if (!studentId) {
            App.showToast("Siswa target belum terpilih!");
            return;
        }

        if (!nama || !poin) {
            App.showToast("Pilih jenis pelanggaran dari daftar resmi terlebih dahulu!");
            return;
        }

        try {
            StorageManager.addViolation(studentId, {
                nama,
                kategori,
                poin: Number(poin),
                tanggal,
                sanksi,
                pencatat: AuthManager.getCurrentUser() ? AuthManager.getCurrentUser().name : "Guru Piket"
            });

            this.closeAllModals();

            if (AuthManager.isTeacher()) {
                AdminPortal.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent(studentId);
            }

            if (AuthManager.isStudent() && AuthManager.getCurrentUser().studentId === studentId) {
                SiswaPortal.renderMyStudentPortal(studentId);
            }

            const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
            App.showToast(`Pelanggaran siswa ${student.nama} (${poin} Poin) berhasil disimpan. Sanksi: ${sanksi}`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message);
        }
    },

    // 2. Submit Prestasi Siswa
    handleAchievementSubmit(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const studentId = document.getElementById("formPrestasiSiswaId").value;
        const peringkat = document.getElementById("formPrestasiJuara").value;
        const tingkat = document.getElementById("formPrestasiTingkat").value;
        const nama = document.getElementById("formPrestasiNama").value.trim();
        const penyelenggara = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPenyelenggara").value.trim();
        const tanggal = document.getElementById("formPrestasiTanggal").value;
        const poin = Number(document.getElementById("formPrestasiPoin").value) || 30;

        // Piagam (Upload File atau URL)
        const piagamUrlInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiPiagamUrl")?.value.trim();
        const piagam = this.uploadedPrestasiPiagamUrl || piagamUrlInput || "";

        // Foto Dokumentasi (Upload File atau URL - Tidak Wajib)
        const fotoUrlInput = document.getElementById("formPrestasiFoto")?.value.trim();
        const foto = this.uploadedPrestasiFotoUrl || fotoUrlInput || piagam || undefined;

        if (!studentId) {
            App.showToast("Siswa target belum terpilih!");
            return;
        }

        if (!peringkat || !tingkat || !nama || !penyelenggara) {
            App.showToast("Lengkapi peringkat juara, tingkat, nama kejuaraan, dan penyelenggara!");
            return;
        }

        const keterangan = `${peringkat} Tingkat ${tingkat} • Penyelenggara: ${penyelenggara}`;

        try {
            StorageManager.addAchievement(studentId, {
                nama,
                kategori: "Akademik",
                tingkat,
                peringkat,
                penyelenggara,
                piagam,
                poin: Number(poin),
                tanggal,
                foto,
                keterangan,
                pencatat: AuthManager.getCurrentUser() ? AuthManager.getCurrentUser().name : "Tim Kesiswaan"
            });

            this.closeAllModals();

            if (AuthManager.isTeacher()) {
                AdminPortal.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                AdminPortal.renderAdminPrestasiList();
                AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent(studentId);
            }

            if (AuthManager.isStudent() && AuthManager.getCurrentUser().studentId === studentId) {
                SiswaPortal.renderMyStudentPortal(studentId);
            }

            // Refresh Slider Prestasi Dasbor Utama
            App.initPrestasiSlider();

            const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(studentId);
            App.showToast(`Prestasi ${student.nama} (${peringkat} ${tingkat} - ${nama}) berhasil disimpan (+${poin} Poin)!`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message);
        }
    },

    // 3. Tambah Siswa Baru & Pengelolaan Foto
    handleAddStudentFotoUpload(e) {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (evt) => {
            this.addStudentCurrentFoto = evt.target.result;
            const preview = document.getElementById("formAddStudentFotoPreview");
            if (preview) preview.src = this.addStudentCurrentFoto;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    },

    handleAddStudentFotoUrlInput(url) {
        if (!url) return;
        this.addStudentCurrentFoto = url.trim();
        const preview = document.getElementById("formAddStudentFotoPreview");
        if (preview) preview.src = this.addStudentCurrentFoto;
    },

    handleAddStudent(e) {
        this.handleStudentSubmit(e);
    },

    handleStudentSubmit(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const nisn = document.getElementById("formStudentNisn").value.trim();
        const nama = document.getElementById("formStudentNama").value.trim();
        const gender = document.getElementById("formStudentGender").value;
        const kelas = document.getElementById("formStudentKelas").value.trim();
        const jurusan = document.getElementById("formStudentJurusan").value.trim();
        const waliKelas = document.getElementById("formStudentWali").value.trim();
        const nis = document.getElementById("formStudentNis") ? document.getElementById("formStudentNis").value.trim() : "";
        const nomorHp = document.getElementById("formStudentNomorHp") ? document.getElementById("formStudentNomorHp").value.trim() : "";
        const spmb = document.getElementById("formStudentSpmb") ? document.getElementById("formStudentSpmb").value.trim() : "";
        const namaBapak = document.getElementById("formStudentNamaBapak") ? document.getElementById("formStudentNamaBapak").value.trim() : "";
        const hpBapak = document.getElementById("formStudentHpBapak") ? document.getElementById("formStudentHpBapak").value.trim() : "";
        const namaIbu = document.getElementById("formStudentNamaIbu") ? document.getElementById("formStudentNamaIbu").value.trim() : "";
        const hpIbu = document.getElementById("formStudentHpIbu") ? document.getElementById("formStudentHpIbu").value.trim() : "";
        const alamat = document.getElementById("formStudentAlamat") ? document.getElementById("formStudentAlamat").value.trim() : "";
        const mapsAlamat = document.getElementById("formStudentMapsAlamat") ? document.getElementById("formStudentMapsAlamat").value.trim() : "";

        const isXiXii = this.studentFormFormat === 'xi_xii' || /^(XI|XII)(\s|$)/i.test(kelas);

        if (isXiXii) {
            if (!nis || !nama) {
                App.showToast("Khusus Kelas XI & XII: NIS dan Nama Lengkap siswa wajib diisi!");
                return;
            }
            if (!kelas) {
                App.showToast("Kelas siswa wajib diisi!");
                return;
            }
        } else {
            if (!nisn || !nama || !kelas) {
                App.showToast("NISN, Nama, dan Kelas wajib diisi untuk Kelas X!");
                return;
            }
        }

        try {
            const newStudent = StorageManager.addStudent({
                foto: this.addStudentCurrentFoto || "",
                nis,
                nisn,
                nama,
                gender,
                kelas,
                jurusan,
                waliKelas,
                nomorHp,
                inputSpmb: spmb,
                namaOrtuBapak: namaBapak,
                noHpBapak: hpBapak,
                namaOrtuIbu: namaIbu,
                noHpIbu: hpIbu,
                alamat,
                mapsAlamat,
                kontakOrtu: hpBapak || hpIbu || "-"
            });

            this.closeAllModals();
            this.populateDropdowns();

            if (AuthManager.isTeacher()) {
                AdminPortal.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent(newStudent.id);
            }

            App.showToast(`Siswa baru ${nama} berhasil ditambahkan.`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message);
        }
    },

    // 3B. EDIT PROFIL SISWA (LENGKAP SESUAI FORMAT EXCEL IMPOR)
    openEditStudentModal(studentId) {
        const id = studentId || (AdminPortal.selectedStudent ? AdminPortal.selectedStudent.id : null);
        if (!id) {
            App.showToast("Pilih siswa yang ingin diedit terlebih dahulu!");
            return;
        }

        const student = StorageManager.getStudentById(id);
        if (!student) {
            App.showToast("Data siswa tidak ditemukan.");
            return;
        }

        this.editStudentCurrentId = student.id;
        this.editStudentCurrentFoto = student.foto || "";

        // Isi field form edit
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentId").value = student.id;
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNis").value = student.nis || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNisn").value = student.nisn || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentGender").value = student.gender || "L";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNama").value = student.nama || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentKelas").value = student.kelas || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentJurusan").value = student.jurusan || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentWali").value = student.waliKelas || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNomorHp").value = student.nomorHp || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentSpmb").value = student.inputSpmb || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNamaBapak").value = student.namaOrtuBapak || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentHpBapak").value = student.noHpBapak || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentNamaIbu").value = student.namaOrtuIbu || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentHpIbu").value = student.noHpIbu || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentAlamat").value = student.alamat || "";
        document.getElementById("formEditStudentMapsAlamat").value = student.mapsAlamat || "";

        const preview = document.getElementById("formEditStudentFotoPreview");
        if (preview) {
            preview.src = student.foto || (student.gender === 'P' 
                ? "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1544005313-94ddf0286df2?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80"
                : "https://images.unsplash.com/photo-1539571696357-5a69c17a67c6?auto=format&fit=crop&w=300&q=80");
        }

        const fileInput = document.getElementById("formEditStudentFotoFile");
        if (fileInput) fileInput.value = "";
        const urlInput = document.getElementById("formEditStudentFotoUrl");
        if (urlInput) urlInput.value = "";

        this.openModal("modalEditStudent");
    },

    handleEditStudentFotoUpload(e) {
        const file = e.target.files[0];
        if (!file) return;
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.onload = (evt) => {
            this.editStudentCurrentFoto = evt.target.result;
            const preview = document.getElementById("formEditStudentFotoPreview");
            if (preview) preview.src = this.editStudentCurrentFoto;
        };
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
    },

    handleEditStudentFotoUrlInput(url) {
        if (!url) return;
        this.editStudentCurrentFoto = url.trim();
        const preview = document.getElementById("formEditStudentFotoPreview");
        if (preview) preview.src = this.editStudentCurrentFoto;
    },

    testOpenMaps() {
        const mapsVal = document.getElementById("formEditStudentMapsAlamat").value.trim();
        const alamatVal = document.getElementById("formEditStudentAlamat").value.trim();
        if (mapsVal) {
            window.open(mapsVal, "_blank");
        } else if (alamatVal) {
            window.open("https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=" + encodeURIComponent(alamatVal), "_blank");
        } else {
            App.showToast("Isi alamat lengkap atau link Google Maps terlebih dahulu!");
        }
    },

    handleSaveEditStudent(e) {
        e.preventDefault();
        const id = document.getElementById("formEditStudentId").value;
        const nisn = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNisn").value.trim();
        const nama = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNama").value.trim();
        const gender = document.getElementById("formEditStudentGender").value;
        const kelas = document.getElementById("formEditStudentKelas").value.trim();
        const jurusan = document.getElementById("formEditStudentJurusan").value.trim();
        const waliKelas = document.getElementById("formEditStudentWali").value.trim();
        const nis = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNis").value.trim();
        const nomorHp = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNomorHp").value.trim();
        const spmb = document.getElementById("formEditStudentSpmb").value.trim();
        const namaBapak = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNamaBapak").value.trim();
        const hpBapak = document.getElementById("formEditStudentHpBapak").value.trim();
        const namaIbu = document.getElementById("formEditStudentNamaIbu").value.trim();
        const hpIbu = document.getElementById("formEditStudentHpIbu").value.trim();
        const alamat = document.getElementById("formEditStudentAlamat").value.trim();
        const mapsAlamat = document.getElementById("formEditStudentMapsAlamat").value.trim();

        const isXiXii = /^(XI|XII)(\s|$)/i.test(kelas);
        if (isXiXii) {
            if (!nis || !nama || !kelas) {
                App.showToast("Khusus Kelas XI & XII: NIS, Nama Lengkap, dan Kelas wajib diisi!");
                return;
            }
        } else {
            if ((!nisn && !nis) || !nama || !kelas) {
                App.showToast("NIS/NISN, Nama, dan Kelas wajib diisi!");
                return;
            }
        }

        try {
            const updated = StorageManager.updateStudent(id, {
                foto: this.editStudentCurrentFoto || "",
                nis,
                nisn,
                nama,
                gender,
                kelas,
                jurusan,
                waliKelas,
                nomorHp,
                inputSpmb: spmb,
                namaOrtuBapak: namaBapak,
                noHpBapak: hpBapak,
                namaOrtuIbu: namaIbu,
                noHpIbu: hpIbu,
                alamat,
                mapsAlamat,
                kontakOrtu: hpBapak || hpIbu || "-"
            });

            this.closeAllModals();

            if (AuthManager.isTeacher()) {
                AdminPortal.renderTeacherAllStudents();
                if (AdminPortal.selectedStudent && AdminPortal.selectedStudent.id === id) {
                    AdminPortal.selectTargetStudent(id);
                }
            }

            App.showToast(`✅ Profil siswa ${updated.nama} berhasil diperbarui.`);
        } catch (err) {
            App.showToast(err.message);
        }
    },
